# AGI Workforce chat inventory

Status: Current
Owner: Repository maintainers
Last updated: 2026-09-04

Read-only map of the web chat product as of commit ce1391c49 on 2026-09-04, built for the
chatgpt.com and claude.ai parity work. It records entry points, components, behaviours,
states, tokens, vendor branches, smells, tests and open questions per area, and nothing
here is a claim about desired behaviour.

## Shell

### Entry points

- `apps/web/proxy.ts:109-146, 178-210, 254-284`: Next middleware. Matches '/' and '/agi-work'; for a signed-in visitor (hasBrowserSessionCookie) rewrites them to /chat (buildHomeResponse/buildAgiWorkResponse -> buildProductRewriteResponse), and '/agi-code' to /chat/code; signed-out gets NextResponse.next() (marketing page untouched). Stamps x-agi-pathname header and a nonce'd CSP on every response. '/chat(.\*)' is in isProtectedAppRoute: no browser session cookie -> 307 redirect to /login?redirectTo=... before Clerk runs.
- `apps/web/app/chat/layout.tsx:1-23`: Server layout for the whole /chat subtree, export const dynamic='force-dynamic'. Re-checks auth() (redirect to /login if no userId, defense-in-depth on top of the proxy gate), reads x-agi-pathname for the post-login redirect target, calls requireCurrentTermsAcceptance(userId, redirectTo), then wraps children in ChatStreamRuntimeProvider.
- `apps/web/app/chat/page.tsx:1-14`: New-chat route. Reads x-agi-pathname to detect an /agi-work rewrite and passes initialWorkMode='agiwork' into WebChatRoot; otherwise no initial mode.
- `apps/web/app/chat/[sessionId]/page.tsx:1-4`: Existing-conversation route, renders bare <WebChatRoot /> (no initialWorkMode); sessionId is read inside WebChatPage via useParams().
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/WebChatRoot.tsx:1-17`: Client wrapper: next/dynamic(() => import(WebChatPage), {ssr:false, loading: ChatLoadingSkeleton}). This is why /chat never server-renders the shell, first paint is always the skeleton, then the client bundle mounts WebChatPage.
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:1-5046 (component body 752-5046)`: The page layout component: 5046-line client component that owns the sidebar wiring, header, message list, composer, and side panels for /chat and /chat/[sessionId]. Default export, props {initialWorkMode?}.
- `apps/web/app/chat/loading.tsx`: Route-level Suspense fallback, renders ChatLoadingSkeleton (bare spinner).
- `apps/web/app/chat/error.tsx`: Client error boundary for the /chat subtree: centered card, 'Chat could not be displayed', Try again (reset()) and 'Back to chat' link (<a href=/chat>), shows error.digest.
- `apps/web/app/chat/not-found.tsx`: Static not-found card: 'Conversation not found' + 'Start a new chat' link to /chat.
- `apps/web/app/chat/projects/page.tsx, /chat/projects/[id]/page.tsx, /chat/library/page.tsx, /chat/schedules/page.tsx, /chat/code/page.tsx (via features/code/CloudCodePage.tsx):library/page.tsx:8,20; projects/page.tsx:143,477; projects/[id]/page.tsx:206-799; schedules/page.tsx:14,16; CloudCodePage.tsx:516,1153`: All adjacent /chat/\* section routes wrap their content in the lighter shared shell <WebAppShell> instead of WebChatPage's own sidebar wiring.
- `apps/web/app/chat/artifacts/page.tsx, apps/web/app/chat/customize/page.tsx`: Retired destinations kept as server redirects: /chat/artifacts -> /chat/library?surface=artifact; /chat/customize -> /settings/{section}.
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:1-501`: Persistent app-shell chrome (sidebar + content) for secondary authenticated surfaces that are NOT the chat page: /chat/projects, /chat/projects/[id], /chat/library, /chat/schedules, /chat/code. Explicitly documented as NOT used by WebChatPage, which keeps its own richer Sidebar wiring (streaming state, dialogs, usage widget).
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/app-nav-items.ts:1-171`: Single shared primary-rail definition (buildAppNavItems) consumed by both WebChatPage and WebAppShell so the nav list and active-state derivation cannot drift between the two shells.

| WebChatPage                                | apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx                        | Owns the whole /chat page: narrow-viewport detection, sidebar (desktop rail + mobile Sheet drawer), header (nav trigger, share, conversation title menu, panel toggles, approval inbox), error/notification banners, empty-vs-active message column switch, composer, and the artifacts/research/work-session side panels. | Tailwind utility classes + --chat-\* CSS custom properties from packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css |
| Sidebar                                    | packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/Sidebar.tsx                              | Shared conversation/project list + primary nav rail, exported from @agiworkforce/ui. Renders as a fixed-width column (prop width, default 260) or a 64px icon rail when collapsed=true. Used by both WebChatPage (desktop rail + inside its Sheet drawer) and WebAppShell.                                                 | Tailwind + --chat-sidebar-bg / --chat-border-subtle tokens                                             |
| WebAppShell                                | apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx                   | Lightweight navigation-focused shell for /chat/projects, /chat/projects/[id], /chat/library, /chat/schedules, /chat/code. ### States covered

default (empty chat, no messages); default (active chat, transcript loaded); loading (route-level cold start, ChatLoadingSkeleton); loading (conversation transcript pending, ChatLoadingState); loading (sidebar conversation list pending); loading (account/billing hydrating in footer); streaming (isStreaming gates composer stop button, work-session panel visibility, research follow-up availability); success (message send, banners cleared); failure (chat send/stream error banner with retry); failure (route render crash, app/chat/error.tsx); empty (no conversation selected); empty (not-found conversation, app/chat/not-found.tsx); overflow (narrow header title truncation via panel-toggle consolidation into one dropdown under sm); mobile (<=768px sidebar becomes drawer; <640px side panels become full-screen overlays); hover (toggle buttons, sidebar rows, account menu trigger, bg-card/60 -> hover:bg-muted/60 etc.); focus (focus-visible ring on account menu triggers, mobile nav trigger, panel resize handle); active/open (panel toggle buttons switch to bg-primary/15 text-primary when their panel is open); disabled (account menu trigger disabled + aria-busy while isAccountLoading)

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:156-165, 172-173 ([data-chat-theme='cool'] selector)`: An opt-in ChatGPT-style ('cool') light/dark palette override layered on top of the default warm/Claude-leaning palette, not vendor-detection per se, but a deliberately named ChatGPT-parity theme branch a reader should know exists before assuming --chat-bg always resolves to the warm value.
- `apps/web/proxy.ts: buildAgiCodeResponse/AGI_CODE_PATH vs buildAgiWorkResponse/AGI_WORK_PATH`: Two differently-branded entry surfaces ('/agi-work' -> /chat with agiwork composer mode, '/agi-code' -> /chat/code) rewritten for signed-in users only, not a third-party vendor branch, but worth flagging as the closest thing to a mode/vendor fork in this area.

### Generic dashboard smells

- None found in this area: the empty-chat state deliberately has no stat tiles, no suggestion chips (explicitly removed per an in-code founder decision dated 2026-08-06), and no generic KPI-card layout, it is brand mark + time-aware greeting + composer only, which is the opposite of a templated-dashboard smell.
- The two side-panel toggle badges (artifact/source/output counts) are the only count-driven chrome, and they render as small unread-style pills on the toggle buttons rather than a dashboard summary row.

### Tests

- apps/web/e2e/chat-layout.spec.ts, composer and message column share the same max-w-3xl edges; model picker popover stays inside the viewport at 1280x600; sidebar conversation list stays scrollable on a short viewport
- apps/web/e2e/chat-sidebar-drawer.spec.ts, mobile (390x844) drawer opens via the shared Sheet, traps focus, Escape closes and returns focus to the trigger, covers listener-order behaviour jsdom cannot reproduce
- apps/web/e2e/qa-05-responsive.spec.ts, apps/web/e2e/marketing-mobile-nav.spec.ts, broader responsive coverage (not chat-shell-specific, not read in depth)
- apps/web/features/chat/pages/**tests**/header-i18n.test.ts, header copy is translated (share button, mobile-nav trigger), Sheet-based drawer markup asserted by source-string checks, referenced i18n keys exist in the English bundle
- apps/web/features/chat/pages/**tests**/WebChatPage.\*.test.tsx (auto-title, concurrent-attachment-send, export, highlight-message, image-persistence, retired-model, shortcuts), behavioural, not layout-focused
- apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.test.tsx, WebAppShell-specific coverage
- apps/web/shared/components/layout/nav-visibility.test.ts, sidebar-session-actions.test.ts, shared rail visibility and session-action confirm-copy coverage
- apps/web/shared/components/**tests**/theme-contrast.test.ts, computes contrast ratios for --chat-bg/--chat-text-\*/--chat-sidebar-bg/--chat-border-strong pairs, including the 'cool' theme branch, straight from the stylesheet
- apps/web/features/chat/components/GreetingBanner/useGreeting.test.ts, greeting headline logic
- apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ChatLoadingState.test.tsx, asserts the loading skeleton occupies the same column as .message-inner

### Open questions

- ChatLoadingSkeleton (apps/web/features/chat/components/ChatLoadingSkeleton.tsx) doesn't use the shared Spinner primitive (packages/ui/ui/src/primitives/Spinner.tsx) called out by .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md; it has its own aria-live/aria-busy/aria-label handling but its inline animation is not covered by the global prefers-reduced-motion .animate-spin rule (apps/web/app/globals.css:2237-2242), worth confirming whether that's an intentional exception (route-level fallback, no store/i18n available yet) or a gap.
- WebChatPage's account-footer loading treatment (aria-busy + disabled button, email hidden) differs visually from WebAppShell's dedicated animate-pulse skeleton block for the same slot, not verified live in a browser, only from source, so it's possible this reads fine but is worth a visual check across both shells side by side.
- Did not verify a genuine 'tablet' intermediate breakpoint distinct from the binary 768px sidebar-drawer split and the 639px panel-overlay split, the responsive behaviour appears to be exactly two step functions (mobile drawer <=768px, panel-overlay <640px) rather than three tiers; not confirmed live in a browser at e.g. 820px (iPad-portrait) width.
- Sidebar expanded width: neither WebChatPage nor WebAppShell pass an explicit `width` prop to the persistent (non-collapsed, non-mobile) <Sidebar>, so it renders at the component's own default of 260px, did not find an override anywhere in this area, but did not exhaustively grep every call site outside apps/web/features/chat and apps/web/shared/components/layout.
- Did not inspect apps/web/app/chat/from-share/[token]/page.tsx or apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx's AGI Work / AGI Code composer-toggle rendering in detail, out of scope for the shell/header/width mapping requested, flagging only that it exists as an entry point.

## Sidebar

### Entry points

- `apps/web/app/chat/page.tsx`: Route that mounts WebChatRoot -> WebChatPage, the real /chat entry point
- `apps/web/app/chat/[sessionId]/page.tsx`: Route for /chat/[id], same WebChatRoot -> WebChatPage mount
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/WebChatRoot.tsx:1-15`: Thin dynamic-import wrapper around WebChatPage
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:93-103, 727-787, 4543-4646`: PRODUCTION call site for /chat. Imports Sidebar/Sheet/useConfirm from '@agiworkforce/ui', wires every session/project handler, owns the mobile drawer (Sheet) and the sidebarCollapsed store selector
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:31-56, 110, 337-500`: SECOND production call site: mounts the same shared Sidebar for every non-/chat route (projects, settings, etc.) with its own, admittedly-thinner handler wiring (comment at line 14: 'The chat page keeps its own richer Sidebar wiring')
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/Sidebar.tsx:1-1243`: THE shared Sidebar component both call sites render, exported as `Sidebar` from '@agiworkforce/ui'. Owns layout, collapsed rail, projects section, chats section, temporal grouping, keyboard roving-tabindex, footer/usage widget
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/SessionItem.tsx:1-322`: One conversation row: title/preview, rename-in-place, hover action row, '...' menu (pin/star/rename/share/move-to-project/archive/delete)
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/Menu.tsx:1-355`: Portalled dropdown primitive used by every '...' menu in the sidebar (row menu, project row menu, 'Organize chats' menu); hand-rolls its own arrow/Home/End/Escape/Tab keyboard handling and pointerdown outside-close
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/SearchOverlay.tsx:1-104`: Fallback in-sidebar search dialog, only rendered when the caller passes no onOpenSearch (dead in production: both call sites always pass onOpenSearch)
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/temporal.ts:1-48`: getTemporalGroup()/toSafeDate() grouping logic (today/yesterday/thisWeek/last7Days/last30Days/older)
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/types.ts:1-58`: SidebarSession/SidebarProject/SidebarNavItem/SidebarTemporalGroup contracts
- `apps/web/shared/stores/layout-store.ts:1-90`: PRODUCTION persisted store for sidebarCollapsed (zustand persist, localStorage key 'agi-ui-store', version 2 with a migrate fn); the only member left in this store per its own doc comment
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/sidebar-session-actions.ts:1-49`: Shared confirm-dialog copy (conversationDeleteConfirm/projectDeleteConfirm) reused by both call sites so the two independently-mounted Sidebars don't drift
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/app-nav-items.ts`: buildAppNavItems(): the single source for the nav rail items (Projects/Skills/etc.) passed as navItems to both Sidebar mounts
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/Sidebar.tsx:1-293`: A SEPARATE, simpler Sidebar implementation in the unified-chat package. Not imported anywhere under apps/web/app or apps/web/features (verified: no production import of ChatInterface/Sidebar from '@agiworkforce/unified-chat'); effectively an unused/parallel component
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/hooks/useSidebar.ts:1-11`: Companion hook for the unused unified-chat Sidebar (reads uiStore.sidebarCollapsed, resolves width from tokens.spacing)
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ConversationItem.tsx:1-60`: Row component for the unused unified-chat Sidebar: no hover actions, no menu, no pin/archive/share, far less capable than the production SessionItem

### States covered

default; hover (row + project row + nav item, revealed action strips); focus (roving-tabindex ring-2 ring-offset-1 on SessionItem via isKeyboardFocused; focus-visible rings on menu items and nav buttons); active (isActive session/project bg-accent + aria-current="page"; active nav item aria-current); disabled (account-menu trigger button disabled+aria-busy while isAccountLoading, disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-70); loading (5-row animate-pulse skeleton with role="status", shown only while list is genuinely still empty); streaming/running (animate-ping pulse dot + sr-only 'Running' on a session whose turn is in flight); success (implicit: optimistic pin/star/archive/rename/move updates render immediately); failure/error (distinct 'Couldn't load conversations' state with the server error message, separate from the empty state; project pin/delete show toast.error and roll back the optimistic store change); empty (no conversations yet / no archived conversations, each with a 'Start a new chat' CTA when not viewing archive); overflow (projects list and per-project chat list Show more/Show less at 5 items; search results capped at 10); mobile (Sheet drawer under 768px width, hamburger trigger, 280px wide, inert background, focus management)

### Vendor branches

- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:4570-4646 vs apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:395-500`: Not a vendor/provider branch but a duplicated-wiring split: the same Sidebar component is independently instantiated once for /chat (richer: usage widget, streaming/running state, custom-instructions) and once for every other route via WebAppShell (thinner). sidebar-session-actions.ts exists specifically to keep the two delete-confirm copies from drifting; app-nav-items.ts keeps the nav rail array from drifting. No other divergence was checked in this pass.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/Sidebar.tsx vs packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`: Two structurally different Sidebar implementations exist in the monorepo. packages/ui/ui's is the one actually rendered in production (verified via import chain from apps/web/app/chat routes). packages/ui/unified-chat's Sidebar/useSidebar/ConversationItem/uiStore is a parallel, less-capable implementation (no menu, no pin/star/archive/share/move actions, different collapsed width 48px vs 64px, different persisted store key) with no production import site found under apps/web/app or apps/web/features.
- `Menu.tsx:135-172 computePosition()`: portalled (default, used by every sidebar menu trigger) vs non-portalled positioning branch; the non-portalled path is a single-shift correction for a caller outside the sidebar (documented as the connectors table), not exercised by sidebar call sites

### Generic dashboard smells

- Two independently-maintained Sidebar mounts (WebChatPage and WebAppShell) for what is supposed to be one shared component; the code's own comments acknowledge the split and the confirm-copy/nav-item files exist only to contain the resulting drift risk.
- packages/ui/unified-chat ships a full second Sidebar/useSidebar/ConversationItem/uiStore stack that is not wired into the production app at all (no import from apps/web/app or apps/web/features) -- effectively dead/parallel code that could mislead future edits if someone patches the wrong Sidebar.
- Sidebar's own SearchOverlay component is permanently dead in production because both call sites always pass onOpenSearch, so the `!onOpenSearch && <SearchOverlay .../>` branch never renders; the shared component still ships and tests it.
- Menu.tsx re-implements arrow/Home/End/Escape/Tab menu keyboard handling inline instead of using the repo's own packages/ui/ui/src/primitives/useMenuKeyboard.ts hook that other menus (ArtifactPanel, ChatInput, GlobalSearchDialog) already use -- worth checking whether this is intentional (sidebar-specific requirements around isMenuPanelOpen()/keepOpenForMenuEscape) or an instance of the exact 'hand-rolling this produced real defects' pattern the ui-colour-and-interaction.md rule warns about.

### Tests

- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Sidebar.layout.test.tsx, width prop / root layout
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Sidebar.collapsedNav.test.tsx, collapsed rail renders every navItems destination
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Sidebar.emptyState.test.tsx, empty-state rendering
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Sidebar.runState.test.tsx, running/streaming pulse indicator
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Sidebar.projects.test.tsx, projects section grouping/overflow
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Sidebar.i18nCatalogue.test.tsx, sidebar.\* keys resolve from the real fr/chat.json catalogue
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Menu.keyboard.test.tsx, menu arrow/Home/End nav
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Menu.placement.test.tsx, viewport flip/clamp positioning
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/**tests**/Menu.drawerEscape.test.tsx, Escape closes only the row menu, not the parent drawer
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/SearchOverlay.test.tsx
- apps/web/shared/components/layout/sidebar-session-actions.test.ts, delete-confirm copy
- apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.test.tsx
- apps/web/shared/stores/layout-store.test.ts, sidebarCollapsed persistence
- apps/web/e2e/chat-sidebar-drawer.spec.ts, real-browser: trigger opens drawer + focus, Escape closes + returns focus, overlay click closes, row menu stays operable inside the drawer, selecting a conversation closes the drawer
- apps/web/e2e/qa-09-menu-keyboard.spec.ts, real-browser menu keyboard contract (the class of bug jsdom cannot catch per the ui-colour-and-interaction.md rule)
- apps/web/e2e/chat-layout.spec.ts
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/**tests**/artifactsSidebarRemoved.test.ts, chatUxShell.test.tsx, tests for the unused parallel Sidebar implementation

### Open questions

- Where is onOpenCustomInstructions wired from WebChatPage/WebAppShell to SessionItem (what UI does it open)? Not traced in this pass.
- Does Menu.tsx's inline keyboard handling deliberately diverge from primitives/useMenuKeyboard.ts, or is it an untracked duplicate that predates that hook? Worth a git-blame comparison of the two files' dates before treating this as a defect.
- Is packages/ui/unified-chat's Sidebar/ChatInterface genuinely dead code (no import anywhere, e.g. Storybook, a different app, or a future surface), or reachable from some entry point outside apps/web that this pass did not search?
- Contrast against `apps/web/e2e/qa-10-overlay-blocking.spec.ts` and `qa-07-interaction-crawl.spec.ts` (both grep-matched 'sidebar' but were not opened) for any additional sidebar-adjacent coverage or gaps.

## Composer

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx`: Root composer component (4623 lines). Owns all state: message text, attachments, mode toggle, tool toggles, slash/mention menus, queued follow-ups, send guard, paste/drop handling, footer disclosure line. Renders ComposerInput and an inline ComposerFooter.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/composer-editor-gate.ts:1-55`: Resolves which editor arm renders: query param ?composer=editor\|textarea overrides localStorage key agi.composer-editor overrides NEXT_PUBLIC_COMPOSER_EDITOR build env (default 'textarea' when unset). All client-only; SSR/first paint always uses the build default via useSyncExternalStore's getServerSnapshot.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerInput.tsx:1-228`: Switches between ComposerTextarea and ComposerRichEditor components based on the gate; owns the shared Tailwind classes (resting height, 240px max-height, text size at empty vs. non-empty state) each arm applies to its own markup.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:1-1217`: Model picker trigger + Popover (roster, search, thinking/effort control, deprecation and provider-degraded badges, tier lock, mid-conversation model-switch AlertDialog) plus StyleSelector. Mounted inline inside ChatComposerNew's control row via <ComposerFooter inline showModelSelector .../>.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/SendButton.tsx:1-84`: Presentational send / stop / queue button. Only 'send' and 'stop' modes are ever passed by ChatComposerNew; 'queue' is dead from this composer's call site.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/SlashCommandMenu.tsx:1-204`: Slash-command suggestion list combining built-in commands, user custom commands (web-settings-store), and skills; exposes an imperative handleKey(key) used by ChatComposerNew's onKeyDown.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/AnchoredComposerMenu.tsx:1-251`: Shared portal/positioning primitive for the plus-menu, mention dropdown, and the image/video aspect-ratio and quality submenus; viewport-clamped and closes on outside click/Escape.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/AttachmentPreview.tsx:1-219`: Renders staged attachment chips (image thumbnails vs. document chips) above the composer box, with a remove button and an optional outbound-destination privacy chip.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/DragDropOverlay.tsx:1-142`: Window-level drag/drop listeners (dragenter/dragleave/dragover/drop) rendering a full-viewport 'Drop files here' overlay; filters by an optional accept list and caps at maxFiles (default 10).
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/composer-editor/ComposerEditor.tsx`: TipTap/ProseMirror editor arm: mention suggestion plugin, max-length extension, submit keymap extension, imperative handle (setText/isEmpty) attached via callback ref.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/composer-editor/extensions/submit-keymap.ts:1-62`: Editor-arm keyboard contract: Enter sends (unless a menu is active or IME composing or send-shortcut isn't 'enter'), Shift-Enter splits block, Mod-Enter (Cmd/Ctrl+Enter) always sends unconditionally of menu-active-for-Enter's shortcut check, ArrowUp/ArrowDown/Tab/Escape delegate to the active menu.
- `packages/platform/utils/src/composerPaste.ts:1-105`: Framework-neutral paste/drop policy shared by web, unified-chat, mobile and the Chrome extension: decideComposerPaste, LARGE_PASTE_THRESHOLD=10000, filesFromDataTransfer.

### States covered

default (resting composer, empty vs. non-empty height/text-size split); hover (plus-menu trigger, model picker trigger, menu rows, attachment remove button, all via hover:bg-muted/\* utilities); focus (composer container gets accent border + ring-2 + shadow-md via isFocused; focus-visible rings on links/buttons in banners); active/pressed (aria-pressed on mode-toggle segments and locked model rows; toggled state styling on plus-menu trigger and MenuToggleRow items); disabled (composerDisabled from disabled\|\|trialExhausted; SendButton disabled when !hasContent\|\|disabled\|\|isSending; model trigger disabled while modelChangePending; file-input disabled; menu rows individually disabled per capability e.g. Deep Research, Run code); loading (model change 'Saving…' label + disabled trigger; screenshot capture 'Capturing…'; provider-availability fetch on popover open; billingPolicyReady gating with 'Checking your plan…' notices); streaming/turn-active (isTurnActive drives SendButton stop mode, queued-follow-up capture instead of immediate send, placeholder text change to 'Reply: sends when the current response finishes', mode-toggle and image/video-exit controls disabled); success (send clears composer state; queued item auto-flushes and is removed from the list on turn completion); failure/error (media availability 'error' state with retry notice; model-save failure toast; secret-detected warning notice; media/model attachment-conflict banners with recovery actions; stranded-queue navigate-away notice distinguishing 'saved as draft' vs 'nothing was sent'); empty (emptyState prop drives a taller rounded-[26px] shell, larger 18px placeholder text, and a shorter resting row height; empty mention/skills list renders 'No matching skills.'/'No matching projects.'; empty connectors list renders 'No connectors connected yet.'); overflow (char counter overflow at the 100,000-char ceiling; attachment/model conflict banners when an attached file exceeds what the selected model can read; queued-message list scrolls as a ul, not clipped); mobile (COMPOSER_COMPACT_MEDIA_QUERY <640px pins a 36px resting height in JS; DESK_ONLY_COMPOSER_FOOTER_KEYS hides web-search/send-preview/privacy/feedback footer entries below md and relocates the mode toggle and send-route disclosure into the plus-menu; ChatComposerNew.mobileFooter.test.tsx and ChatComposerNew.density.test.tsx cover this)

### Vendor branches

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:1053-1060 (isWebSearchAvailable) and 1065-1071 (providerHasNativeCodeExecution list ['anthropic','google','openai'])`: Whether the ambient web-search status and the Run-code plus-menu toggle are available, branching on the selected model's provider string and its models.json capability flags rather than a single generic 'tools' flag.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:373-411 (ProviderLogo / toProviderId / providerBrandHex)`: Which provider mark renders in the model-picker trigger and rows: a special AGI mark for managed_cloud (Auto modes), else the shared ProviderMark component when hasProviderMark(providerKey) is true, else a local /providers/<id>.svg with a brand-hex fallback dot.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:221-250 (modelLock)`: A single combined lock decision -- availability ('coming_soon') first, then tier, then requiresEnvironment -- consumed by every call site (search, partition, inline 'More models', reset effect) so no one branch can drift from another; comment flags this as a fix for a prior partial-update gating leak.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:255-264 (isHighUsageRateModel)`: Whether a model row shows the 'consumes usage limits faster' tooltip, now sourced from getPickerModelTier(model.id) instead of a prior display-name substring match on the word 'opus' (called out in-code as AUDIT-FIX CMP-25).

### Generic dashboard smells

- Not applicable in the literal sense -- this is a chat composer, not an admin/metrics dashboard -- but the closest analogues are: (1) the model popover's 'Available'/'More models' partition and capability badges are catalog-driven generic list rendering rather than hand-picked marketing copy, which is good; (2) the footer disclosure row is a growing flat array of heterogeneous status/legal/link entries (web-search, memory, send-preview, accuracy, privacy, feedback) joined by string separators computed at render time -- functionally sound per the in-code comments but structurally similar to a generic 'stat strip' that will need another one-off breakpoint rule each time an entry is added.

### Open questions

- SendButton's 'queue' mode (Clock icon, terra-cotta styling) is fully implemented in SendButton.tsx but ChatComposerNew's sendButtonMode is a strict isTurnActive?'stop':'send' ternary -- confirm whether any other composer surface actually mounts SendButton with mode='queue', or whether this is dead code / a mouse-accessibility gap for queuing during a stream (Enter key still works).
- apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/StyleSelector.tsx, VoiceInputButton.tsx, VoiceRecordingOverlay.tsx, and ComposerFeedbackDialog.tsx were only read by reference/grep, not fully audited line-by-line -- their internal state machines (recording states, style-picker options) are not covered here.
- The submit-keymap's Mod-Enter branch always submits regardless of resolveSendShortcut(), while plain Enter respects it; no current caller passes ComposerEditor a non-default sendShortcut, so this divergence is currently dormant but worth flagging if a send-shortcut preference is ever wired up.
- ComposerFooter.tsx is 1217 lines and was not read in full (roughly lines 1-660 and 930-1217 were read closely; 660-930 and end-of-file sections beyond 1213 were sampled via grep rather than read verbatim) -- StyleSelector integration details and the very end of the file were not verified line-by-line.

## Transcript

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ChatMessageList.tsx:1-1909`: Transcript container: groups messages, drives react-window virtualization, owns all auto-scroll/scroll-suppression state, renders the footer notices (Continue/Stopped/StreamError/Refusal/IncompleteTurn), typing indicator, follow-ups, date dividers, and the sr-only live region.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:1-2892`: Per-message renderer: user bubble vs assistant flat column, action row (copy/edit/pin/read-aloud/thumbs/regenerate/branch/more), inline edit, attachments, thinking/tool/artifact/image/video sub-blocks, per-message notices (fallback, secret-redaction).
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:3763-4013, 4853-4935`: Mounts ChatMessageList with all handlers; decides loading-skeleton vs empty-state vs list; owns handleRegenerateMessage (always resends under activeModelId, no model picker) and the inline-edit controller (destructive replace vs new-variant sibling depending on the variants flag).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/VariantPager.tsx:1-77`: index/total pager UI (prev/next chevrons + sr-only live-region announcement), rendered inside the action row for any message with siblings.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/EditableMessage.tsx:1-152`: The inline textarea a bubble becomes while editing (Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to save, Esc to cancel, char-limit hint).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/TranscriptNotice.tsx:1-95`: Shared framed/bare notice primitive used for every failure/incomplete-turn/no-search-results notice.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/messageActionRow.ts:1-13`: Shared sizing tokens (44px mobile / 32px desktop touch targets) for every action-row control including the variant pager.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/TypingIndicator.tsx:1-20`: Three-dot 'Preparing' placeholder shown between send and first token.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ChatLoadingState.tsx:1-41`: Full-transcript skeleton shown while a conversation's history is still loading (bottom-anchored).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubbleSkeleton.tsx:1-64`: Single skeleton row (user-bubble or multi-line assistant) used by ChatLoadingState.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageSearch.tsx:1-151`: Cmd/Ctrl+F in-transcript search overlay (query, match count, prev/next, Escape to close).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/variantDeleteConfirm.ts:1-40`: Builds the confirm-dialog copy for deleting a response and everything that followed it.
- `packages/contracts/cloud-contracts/src/message-thread.ts:1-334`: The parent_id/active-leaf data model: resolveVisibleThread, siblingGroup, variantInfoByMessage, deepestDescendant, subtreeIds, shared by web/desktop/mobile.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/message-variants-gate.ts:1-66`: Feature flag (NEXT_PUBLIC_MESSAGE_VARIANTS, default on) gating whether edit/regenerate create a sibling variant or destructively replace.

### States covered

default; hover (user action row reveal, attachment zoom, dropdown items); focus/focus-visible (group-focus-within reveals the user action row; Button primitive's focus-visible:ring-2); active/toggled (aria-pressed on thumbs/pin/read-aloud with a colour+fill change, no CSS :active rule); disabled (VariantPager prev/next at the ends or while streaming; search prev/next with zero matches; Button primitive disabled:opacity-50); loading (TypingIndicator; ChatLoadingState/MessageBubbleSkeleton for a pending transcript); streaming (isStreaming: hides the action row body, shows 'Thinking...', StreamingMarkdownContent, per-token virtualization stays keyed off the identity, not the flag); success (completed turn, full action row); failure (Stopped / StreamError / Refusal / IncompleteTurn notices, each with its own tone/copy/action); empty (no messages: GreetingBanner; no search matches: 'No matches'); overflow (user bubble >320px collapses with Show more/less; message text break-words/overflow-wrap-anywhere); mobile (44px vs 32px action targets via ACTION_BUTTON_SIZE; action row wraps with flex-wrap)

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:150-224 (html:not(.dark) [data-chat-theme='cool'])`: Opt-in ChatGPT-style ('cool') light palette vs. the default warm/Claude-leaning palette that both MessageBubble and ChatMessageList read exclusively through --chat-\* variables -- no vendor branch lives in the TSX itself, it is entirely a token swap.
- `ChatMessageList.tsx:60-71 (isRefusalFinish)`: Normalizes two different provider wire vocabularies for a safety stop -- the canonical 'refusal' and the OpenAI-wire-compatible 'content_filter' -- into the same single refusal notice, so the UI never branches on which provider produced the turn.

### Generic dashboard smells

- TypingIndicator.tsx hand-rolls three animate-bounce divs instead of using the shared Spinner primitive the project's own .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md requires for loading affordances -- no role/aria-live and no prefers-reduced-motion guard (TypingIndicator.tsx:9-20).
- The five transcript-footer notices (Continue/Stopped/StreamError/Refusal/IncompleteTurn) and the DateDivider use px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20 with no max-w-3xl/mx-auto, while every sibling in the same footer -- the message bubbles above them and the FollowUpSuggestions wrapper right below (which IS asserted by a test to carry mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4) -- is capped and centered at 768px. On a wide viewport these notices would not share the message column's left/right edge.
- The bare TypingIndicator has no width-constraining wrapper at all in the footer (no px-4, no max-w, no mx-auto), unlike every other footer element, so it would render flush at the row's left edge rather than aligned under the message column.
- apps/web/features/chat/chat-interface.css defines a competing .message-bubble { animation: slideIn ... } rule that duplicates and disagrees with globals.css's .message-bubble { animation: messageIn ... }; the file is not imported anywhere in apps/web (grep found zero import sites), so it is dead but could confuse a future edit that assumes it is live.

### Open questions

- Regenerate has no per-click model choice anywhere in this surface (icon button always resends under the conversation's current activeModelId); confirm with the task owner whether 'regenerate with model choice' was meant to exist here or is being planned separately, since nothing in ChatMessageList/MessageBubble/WebChatPage wires one.
- Whether the px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20 gutter on the transcript-footer notices/DateDivider (vs. the fixed px-4 max-w-3xl mx-auto message column) is an intentional wider band or an unnoticed drift -- worth a live check on a >=1024px viewport with a Stopped/StreamError notice showing.
- Whether TypingIndicator's missing width wrapper and lack of an aria-live/role is a known gap or worth a quick fix given the project's own rule explicitly bans hand-rolled loading spinners of this shape.
- Whether apps/web/features/chat/chat-interface.css (unimported, dead) should be deleted outright to remove the conflicting duplicate .message-bubble animation rule.

## Renderer

### Entry points

- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/MarkdownContent.tsx:30-407`: Canonical chat markdown renderer (react-markdown + remark/rehype pipeline, memoized). Shared by web, mobile and desktop.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/StreamingMarkdownContent.tsx:58-117`: Streaming wrapper: splits content into 'settled' finished blocks (rendered once, never re-diffed) plus a live tail (re-rendered every token) via splitMarkdownBlocks, then feeds each through MarkdownContent with skipPreprocess.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/splitMarkdownBlocks.ts:75-204`: unified/remark-parse-based block splitter used only for streaming; determines which top-level mdast nodes are safe to freeze as 'settled' (keeps last 2 nodes as a tail reserve, tracks raw-HTML tag balance, detects reference/footnote definitions).
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/remarkPlugins.ts:9-13`: Single source of truth for the remark plugin list, shared between the renderer and the streaming splitter so both parse identically.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/markdownSanitizeSchema.ts:1-28`: rehype-sanitize schema extending hast-util-sanitize defaultSchema.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/preprocessMath.ts:1`: Re-exports normalizeMathDelimiters from @agiworkforce/utils/markdown-source; converts \( \) / \[ \] to $ $ / $$ $$ before remark-math runs.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/completeInlineTokens.ts:122-129`: Repairs an in-flight streaming tail's unterminated inline tokens (open code-span backticks, an open \*\* bold run, an unterminated markdown link so a half-typed link doesn't render as literal brackets) so the tail parses cleanly every token.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/HighlightedCode.tsx:14-62`: Async Shiki syntax highlighting for fenced code, applied after paint (outside the synchronous react-markdown pipeline) and gated by isStreamTail so the streaming block never highlights mid-token.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/shikiHighlighter.ts:1-265`: Shiki core highlighter: per-language dynamic imports, github-light/github-dark themes, LRU line cache (32 entries), 100k-char / 1k-line-length caps to keep tokenising off long pastes.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/MermaidDiagram.tsx:112-243`: Renders ```mermaid fences via the mermaid package (strict security level, htmlLabels off), sanitizes the resulting SVG through the shared ArtifactRenderer sanitizeSvg, caches by source, gates compilation on bracket-balance while streaming.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/codeBlock.css:1-46`: Base-layer styles for .code-block-container / -header-bar / -lang-label / -body and .shiki-token, all reading --chat-code-_ and --shiki-_ CSS variables.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:88-99, 1707-1760`: Web message bubble; dynamically imports MarkdownContent/StreamingMarkdownContent from @agiworkforce/unified-chat and wraps them in a `.prose .message-text` div.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx:180-412, 792-798`: Desktop/mobile-shared message bubble. Also exports a second, hand-rolled lightweight markdown renderer, MarkdownLite (regex-based, no remark/rehype), used only by PresentationArtifact and EmailArtifact, not by the main chat body.
- `apps/web/app/globals.css:988-1200`: .prose / .message-text base typography, .code-block-\* CSS, and the data-chat-text-size / data-chat-font / data-code-block-wrap preference overrides.
- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:1-330`: --chat-code-_, --chat-font-_, --chat-accent-_, --chat-text-_ token definitions for :root (light/warm-default), .dark, and the opt-in [data-chat-theme='cool'] (ChatGPT-style) light palette; plus all .mermaid-\* diagram styling.

Detects citation-marker hrefs and renders a CitationChip instead of a plain link; strips tracking params; refuses protocol-relative hrefs; keeps same-page (#fragment) links from opening a new tab.                                                              | text-primary Tailwind class for plain links; CitationChip owns its own token-based styling.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| HighlightedCode                                | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/HighlightedCode.tsx         | Reads a synchronous cache (readHighlightCache) for first paint, then async-resolves Shiki tokens via highlightToLines and re-renders as <span class="shiki-token"> runs; falls back to plain <code>{code}</code> when disabled, ungrammared, or the highlighter rejects.                         | .shiki-token / .dark .shiki-token rules in codeBlock.css, driven by inline --shiki-light/--shiki-dark custom properties Shiki itself emits per token.                                                                                                                             |

'ready' shows a Show/Hide-source toggle; 'failed' always shows the raw source with an inline reason. | .mermaid-\* rules in packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css, all keyed off --chat-surface-elevated / --chat-border-strong / --chat-text-primary / --chat-text-muted / --chat-bg so the diagram follows the active palette.                                                        |
| MarkdownLite                                   | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx:182-412            | A second, independent hand-rolled markdown implementation (line-based block splitter + one inline regex pass) used only by PresentationArtifact and EmailArtifact slide/email bodies, NOT the chat message body path, not sanitized via rehype-sanitize, no KaTeX/mermaid/Shiki.                 | Inline Tailwind + --chat-\* tokens duplicated ad hoc (its own CodeBlock, header classes, list classes) rather than reusing codeBlock.css.                                                                                                                                         |

### States covered

default (rendered markdown, code idle); hover (copy button hover-bg/hover-fg tokens; mermaid caption button hover; link hover:underline); focus (focus-visible ring + ring-offset on the shared Button primitive used by the copy button, inherited, not custom-drawn); active/pressed (Button primitive's default variant press styling, inherited); disabled (Button primitive disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none, inherited by the copy button though never actually set to disabled in CodeBlock); loading (image loading skeleton pulse; mermaid 'rendering'/'pending' phase with a reserved-height placeholder and 'Drawing diagram…' status text); streaming (StreamingMarkdownContent settled/tail split; blinking caret span; HighlightedCode/MermaidDiagram both gated off while isStreamTail/isStreaming); success (copy button 'Copied' + Check icon, 2s timeout; image loaded state); failure (copy button 'Copy failed' text, 4s timeout, on clipboard rejection; image error fallback chip with ImageOff icon + alt text; mermaid 'failed' phase showing the reason plus raw source); empty (StreamingThinkingStatus shown by the caller when isStreaming and content is empty, in MessageBubble.tsx not MarkdownContent itself; mermaid looksComplete() returns false for an empty/whitespace-only source and stays pending); overflow (table wrapper overflow-x-auto; code-block pre overflow-x-auto by default, switched to overflow-x: visible + wrap only when data-code-block-wrap='on'; mermaid-diagram overflow-x-auto); mobile (@media max-width:375px .prose font-size 14px; codeBlockWrap and text-size preferences apply identically on mobile since they key off html[data-\*] attributes, not a media query)

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:172-217, selector `html:not(.dark) [data-chat-theme='cool'], html:not(.dark)[data-chat-theme='cool']``: Swaps the code-block, text and accent token values from the warm/Claude-leaning default palette to a ChatGPT-style cool palette (light mode only, no dark counterpart exists for 'cool').
- `apps/web/app/globals.css:1137-1150, `html[data-chat-font='serif'|'sans'|'dyslexic'] .prose``: Overrides the prose font-family (and forces code back to monospace) per a user-selected reading-preference vendor-agnostic setting, unrelated to the ChatGPT/Claude theme branch.
- `shikiHighlighter.ts:117-124, THEME_LOADERS = { light: github-light, dark: github-dark }`: Which of two fixed Shiki themes tokens resolve against; not tied to data-chat-theme='cool', so Shiki token colours do NOT follow the cool/warm chat palette, only the light/dark mode.

### Generic dashboard smells

- MarkdownLite (packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx:182-412) is a second, hand-rolled markdown parser/renderer that duplicates code-block, table, list and link logic already solved by MarkdownContent, a second surface to keep in sync, with no sanitization pass and no KaTeX/mermaid/Shiki support, currently scoped to two artifact bodies (PresentationArtifact, EmailArtifact) rather than chat messages.
- Inline (non-fenced) code styling in MarkdownContent.tsx:60-66 uses raw Tailwind gray-100/gray-800 literals instead of a --chat-code-\* token, unlike every other code-related surface in this area which is token-driven, this is the kind of hardcoded literal the project's lean-code convention flags.
- The streaming caret indicator in MarkdownContent.tsx:400 uses `bg-primary` (a generic Tailwind/shadcn token) rather than a --chat-accent-\* token, breaking the token-role convention documented in .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md for this exact directory tree.
- No line-numbers feature exists for code blocks despite the task's checklist expecting one, worth confirming with the design/product owner whether this is an intentional omission or a gap.

### Tests

- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/MarkdownContent.test.tsx, streaming re-render budget (memo bail-out, preprocessMath not re-run on caret-only changes, plugin arrays allocated once, re-parses when content actually grows)
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/**tests**/StreamingMarkdownContent.test.tsx, settled/tail equivalence to one full parse, reference-link/footnote definitions arriving late, mermaid never flashing a failure state mid-stream, streaming gate reaching both the mermaid compiler and the Shiki highlighter, per-token cost staying off the highlighter
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/splitMarkdownBlocks.test.ts, universal equivalence property, tail-node reserve, list-still-open handling, stable identity/keys across reset, prefix-stability guard, reference/footnote-definition boundary safety, raw-HTML tag-balance (including void/self-closing tags)
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/markdownSanitizeSchema.test.ts, data:/blob: image sources allowed, href protocols NOT widened, language/math className allow-list preserved
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/completeInlineTokens.test.ts, closes open backtick/bold/link tokens in a streaming tail, leaves already-finished markdown untouched
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/preprocessMath.test.ts, \( \) / \[ \] to $ / $$ conversion, code-span and fenced-code exclusion, scanner parity fuzz test, linear-time guarantee on unterminated fences/delimiters
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/**tests**/HighlightedCode.test.tsx, disabled path never calls the highlighter, plain-text-first paint then token replacement, ungrammared language and highlighter-rejection fallbacks, cache-hit dedup
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/**tests**/MermaidDiagram.test.tsx, draws on parseable source, output passed through the package SVG sanitizer, keeps source + states a reason on parse failure, defers on unfinished streaming source, recompiles only on source change
- apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/markdownXss.test.tsx, strips executable HTML, keeps fenced-code language classes, KaTeX rendering for both $ and \( \)/\[ \] delimiter forms including inside inline code, syntax highlighting still works alongside math
- apps/web/shared/components/**tests**/theme-contrast.test.ts, computes WCAG contrast ratios directly from the chat.css stylesheet for every --chat-\*-text / on-fill token pairing

### Open questions

- Whether MarkdownLite's lack of rehype-sanitize is an accepted risk given its current callers (PresentationArtifact, EmailArtifact slide/email bodies are model-authored content) or an oversight, it was not in scope to trace whether those bodies can carry raw HTML from the model.
- Whether the missing dark-mode variant of [data-chat-theme='cool'] is intentional (cool theme is light-only by design) or an unfinished token set, chat.css has no `.dark [data-chat-theme='cool']` block at all.
- Whether line-number support for code blocks is planned; no trace of it (implementation, token, or even a dead flag) exists anywhere under packages/ui/unified-chat/markdown, codeBlock.css, or the appearance-preferences surface.

## Tools

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:123, 1599, 1617, 1669`: Imports ToolTimeline and mounts it three times per assistant message: once per interleaved thinking segment's trailing tool group, once for any tools left over after the last segment, and once on the legacy (non-interleaved) path for `message.metadata.tools`. Every mount spreads `approvalHandlers` (the `onApprove`/`onReject` pair from `resolveToolApproval`, MessageBubble.tsx:769) and `connectRetryHandler` (`onRetryTurn`, wired to the same resend mechanism Regenerate uses, MessageBubble.tsx:780-782) onto ToolTimeline.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ToolTimeline.tsx:1-847`: The general per-tool-call renderer for every tool call on every assistant message (file reads/writes/edits, shell/code execution, MCP connector calls, web search, tool approvals). Not search-specific and not legacy; it is the only tool-call rendering path the production web chat surface mounts. Exports `humanizeToolName` (maps a raw tool id + args to a human label) and `isWebSearchTool`, both re-used outside the timeline itself (see below).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:1-72`: Thin web-local re-export of the package component below; adapts the web app's own `ToolCall`/`ToolCallStatus` shape (pending/running/complete/error/awaiting_approval/cancelled) onto the package's prop names (`args`→`parameters`, `elapsedMs`→`durationMs`, etc.).
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:1-577`: Canonical tool-call card: request/response bodies (JSON, unified-diff, or syntax-highlighted code block depending on `detectCodeBlock`/`detectFileDiff`/`detectResultDiff`), the approval prompt (Approve/Reject buttons, lines 429-448) and the expired-approval prompt (Resend button, lines 405-418), a Cancel action while running, a Copy action, and `humanizeToolErrorText` (strips trust-fence/sealed-envelope wrappers, stack frames, and truncates at 500 chars). Delegates the actual collapse/expand shell and status icon to `InlineToolCall.tsx`.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/InlineToolCall.tsx:1-523`: The collapsible row primitive ToolCallCard renders into: status icon (spinner/check/alert/slash), badge or lucide icon style, keyboard-toggleable expand/collapse body, per-status text color.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ToolTimeline.tsx:475-525` (`ToolPermissionQuickPicker`): Per-tool-call "Remember: Always allow / Ask / Block" quick-pick row shown under an MCP connector call awaiting approval, backed by `useToolPermissionsStore` (persists the decision per server+tool).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/approvals/ApprovalInbox.tsx:1-289`: The tool-approval action row proper: a fixed popover aggregating every pending approval across the visible conversation (not just the latest message), each item showing the humanized tool name (via `ToolTimeline.tsx`'s exported `humanizeToolName`, line 9), formatted input, an optional guidance textarea, and Approve/Reject buttons (lines 242-266). Mounted twice in `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:4736, 4765` (desktop and mobile layout branches).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/work-session/WorkSessionPanel.tsx:31`: A third consumer of `ToolTimeline.tsx`'s `humanizeToolName`, for labeling steps in the background work-session progress panel.
- `apps/web/features/connectors/lib/mcp-tool-name.ts`: `parseQualifiedMcpToolName`/`describeMcpTool`, used by ToolTimeline to detect a `server::tool`-qualified MCP call and render its human-readable server+tool label and Plug icon instead of the generic name-pattern icon.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/connector-connect-required.ts` + `apps/web/features/chat/components/ConnectorConnectCard.tsx`: `readConnectorConnectRequest` inspects a tool call's result/error for a verified "connect required" envelope; when present, ToolTimeline renders an inline `ConnectorConnectCard` under that step instead of (or alongside) the raw result, and suppresses the raw result/error via `withoutRawOutput` (ToolTimeline.tsx:355-360).
- Secondary, dev-only: `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/InlineToolCall.tsx` and `InlineToolCallGroup.tsx` are imported live in apps/web only by `apps/web/app/dev/inline-toolcall-demo/page.tsx`, not by the production chat surface, the same "built in the shared package but not mounted in apps/web" pattern documented for ModelSelector.tsx and AgentControl.tsx under Models. `InlineToolCall.tsx` is still reachable in production indirectly, as the primitive ToolCallCard renders into (see above); `InlineToolCallGroup.tsx` is not reachable at all outside the demo page.

| ToolCallCard (package)    | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx            | Renders the Request/Response/Error sections (JSON, diff, or highlighted code), the approval prompt, expired-approval prompt with Resend, Cancel and Copy actions, elapsed-time ticker while running.                                                                                                                      | Tailwind, mostly raw literals (yellow-50/yellow-900 approval prompt, green-700 Approve button, red-400 error text) rather than the --chat-warning-\*/--chat-destructive-text tokens chat.css defines for exactly these roles |

| ApprovalInbox             | apps/web/features/chat/components/approvals/ApprovalInbox.tsx       | Cross-message pending-approval popover: humanized tool name + formatted input per pending call, optional guidance textarea (capped at TOOL_APPROVAL_GUIDANCE_MAX_LENGTH), Approve/Reject actions, jump-to-message.                                                                                                        | n/a (not inventoried in this pass)                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ConnectorConnectCard      | apps/web/features/chat/components/ConnectorConnectCard.tsx          | Inline "connect this integration to continue" card rendered under a tool step whose result/error carries a verified connect-required envelope.                                                                                                                                                                            | n/a (not inventoried in this pass)                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Behaviours

| Behaviour                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Where                                                                                                                                     | Status      | Note                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Compact mode: once a message has more than 3 tool calls and none are running/awaiting-approval/connect-gated, the timeline collapses to one summary line the user must click to expand.                                                                                              | ToolTimeline.tsx:574-577 (isCompact), 615-633 (collapsed render)                                                                          | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| While any tool is running, the timeline auto-opens and shows the running tool's live status phrase (defaults to "Working...") instead of the summary.                                                                                                                                | ToolTimeline.tsx:594-597 (runningPhrase), 659-661 (header render)                                                                         | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| A user-initiated collapse (clicking the header while open) is remembered as `userForcedClosed` and does not reopen on its own; it resets automatically the next time a running tool transitions to not-running.                                                                      | ToolTimeline.tsx:579-585 (auto-reset effect), 599-607 (handleToggle)                                                                      | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| MCP connector calls are detected via server::tool qualified names and get a Plug icon, a humanized "Server · Tool" label, and (if awaiting approval) the Remember quick-picker instead of the generic Approve/Reject prompt alone.                                                   | ToolTimeline.tsx:26-29 (parseQualifiedMcpToolName), 383 (mcpTool), 396 (showPermissionPicker)                                             | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| Web-search tool steps render as a plain text label (no expandable request/response box, since there is no useful payload) plus a "N source(s)" count when `searchSources` are attached to that step.                                                                                 | ToolTimeline.tsx:410-413 (comment explains the no-box choice), 414-416 (label render), 447-451 (source count)                             | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| Parallel tool calls sharing a `parallelGroup` id render inside a visually distinct bordered block with a "parallel" label and a GitBranch icon, instead of the plain sequential list.                                                                                                | ToolTimeline.tsx:711-754                                                                                                                  | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| A connect-required tool result renders an inline ConnectorConnectCard and hides the raw result/error text for that step (`withoutRawOutput`).                                                                                                                                        | ToolTimeline.tsx:355-360 (withoutRawOutput), 439-446 (connectRequest render)                                                              | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| Approve/Reject on a `ToolCallCard` in awaiting_approval state; an expired approval instead shows a neutral notice with a Resend action.                                                                                                                                              | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:403-419 (expired notice, Resend), 422-450 (approval prompt, Approve/Reject)      | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| A separate, cross-message ApprovalInbox popover aggregates every pending approval in the visible conversation (not just the newest message) with its own Approve/Reject actions and a guidance textarea.                                                                             | apps/web/features/chat/components/approvals/ApprovalInbox.tsx:153 (trigger), 242-266 (actions)                                            | implemented | Duplicates the per-step approve/reject already on ToolCallCard; both act on the same `resolveToolApproval` handler passed down from MessageBubble.tsx:769. |
| Request/response bodies auto-detect and render as a unified diff (FileDiffBlock), a syntax-highlighted code block (execute_code/code_execute/run_code/computer/jupyter_execute and any name containing "execute"/"run_code"/"computer"), or a raw JSON dump, in that priority order. | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:42-49 (CODE_EXECUTION_TOOLS), 51-73 (detectCodeBlock), 452-470 (render priority) | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| Tool error text is humanized before display: strips a leading "Tool error:" prefix, strips trust-fence/sealed-envelope XML wrappers, decodes HTML entities, drops stack-frame lines, and truncates at 500 characters.                                                                | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:311-320 (humanizeToolErrorText)                                                  | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| Copy action serializes `{tool, args, status}` to the clipboard and shows a 2-second "Copied" confirmation via the Check icon.                                                                                                                                                        | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:384-393 (handleCopy), 551-565 (Copy button)                                      | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| A live elapsed-time ticker (100ms interval) runs while a tool is `running`; once complete, the card falls back to the server-reported `elapsedMs`.                                                                                                                                   | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:350-357 (ticker effect), 377-382 (durationLabel fallback)                        | implemented |                                                                                                                                                            |
| `InlineToolCallGroup.tsx` (nested/grouped inline tool calls with an integration-name header) exists in the shared package but is not imported anywhere under apps/web/features/chat; only reachable via the /dev/inline-toolcall-demo route.                                         | packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/InlineToolCallGroup.tsx:1-94                                                                      | missing     | Same "built but not mounted" pattern as ModelSelector.tsx/AgentControl.tsx (see Models).                                                                   |

### States covered

pending (no icon/box yet, ToolCallCard defaults to a neutral pending status); running (spinner icon, live elapsed-time ticker, header shows the running status phrase); completed/complete (check icon, static elapsed duration, Copy action revealed on hover/focus); failed/error (alert icon, red error-count badge in the collapsed header, humanized error text body, no Approve/Reject); awaiting_approval (yellow-tinted prompt with Approve/Reject, or the Remember quick-picker for MCP calls); awaiting_approval + expired (neutral "expired or no longer active" notice with a Resend button in place of Approve/Reject); cancelled (mapped to the InlineToolCall "partial" visual state); connect-required (inline ConnectorConnectCard replaces the raw result, `withoutRawOutput` strips the payload); compact/collapsed (single summary line, chevron, error-count badge); parallel-group (bordered sub-block with a "parallel" label); empty (`tools.length === 0` renders nothing, ToolTimeline.tsx:613); copied (2-second "Copied" confirmation after the Copy action); reduced motion (`useReducedMotion` at ToolTimeline.tsx:611 disables the expand/collapse height+opacity animation, lines 684-694, and the chevron rotation transition, lines 672-673).

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:42-49` (`CODE_EXECUTION_TOOLS`): Recognizes `computer` (Anthropic's computer-use tool id) alongside generic `execute_code`/`code_execute`/`run_code`/`jupyter_execute` names, normalizing all of them to the same syntax-highlighted code-block rendering regardless of which provider emitted the tool call. This is the only provider-name-aware branch inside the tool-rendering components themselves.
- Per-provider tool-call wire shapes are normalized upstream, before reaching this UI: `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:1032` builds the client-side `MessageToolEntry[]` timeline (type at `apps/web/shared/stores/web-chat-store.ts:377`) from an already OpenAI-compatible SSE stream (see `packages/ai/provider-protocol/src/openai-wire-compat.ts` under Search), so ToolTimeline and ToolCallCard never branch on anthropic-shape vs openai-shape tool_use/function_call payloads directly.

### Generic dashboard smells

- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ToolCallCard.tsx:422-450`: The approval prompt (`bg-yellow-50 dark:bg-yellow-950/20`, `text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400`) and Approve button (`bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800`) use raw Tailwind color literals instead of the `--chat-warning-bg`/`--chat-warning-fg` tokens chat.css already defines for exactly this "needs attention" role; the error text at line 493 (`text-red-400`) likewise bypasses `--chat-destructive-text`. `InlineToolCall.tsx`, the primitive this same card delegates its collapse/expand shell to, is fully token-driven (`--chat-destructive-text`, `--chat-warning-fg`), so the inconsistency is local to ToolCallCard's own body markup, not a project-wide gap.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ToolTimeline.tsx:628, 665` (error-count badge, `text-rose-400`) and `:715-719` (parallel-group block, `border-blue-500/30`/`text-blue-400/70`) are further raw-literal color usages in the same file that has token-driven neighbors elsewhere in this document; matches the pattern .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md calls out for MarkdownContent.tsx's streaming caret under Renderer.
- ApprovalInbox.tsx duplicates Approve/Reject affordances that already exist per-step on ToolCallCard/ToolPermissionQuickPicker, worth confirming with product whether the cross-message inbox is meant to fully replace inline approval or coexist with it; not inventoried further in this pass.

### Tests

- apps/web/features/chat/components/ToolCallCard.test.ts, `detectCodeBlock` (execute_code/code_execute variants, non-code tools, missing-language fallback to python)
- apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ToolTimeline.test.tsx, expand/collapse, auto-expand + userForcedClosed reset on run completion, header metadata (running phrase, duration), ToolCallCard rendering, parallel groups, edge cases (empty tools array), audit-trail collapse lifecycle, manual approval (Approve/Reject callbacks), expired approval (Resend), connector authorization required (ConnectorConnectCard render, onRetryTurn wiring, raw-output suppression)
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/**tests**/MessageList.toolApproval.test.tsx, approval prop plumbing through the shared package's own MessageList

### Open questions

- Whether ApprovalInbox's cross-message action row is meant to be the primary approval surface (with the per-step ToolCallCard prompt as a redundant fallback) or the reverse, both are fully wired to the same `resolveToolApproval` handler with no apparent precedence or dedup between them.
- Whether InlineToolCallGroup.tsx (grouped/nested inline tool calls) is intended for a future apps/web surface or is dead code kept alive only by the /dev demo page and its own package tests, no trace of an in-progress integration was found.

## Artifacts

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:924-1000, 1231, 1945-1953`: Detects artifacts in an assistant message's markdown (extractArtifacts / extractCodeBlocks), strips completed fences from the visible text (removeArtifactBlocks), tracks the live-streaming trailing fence (extractTrailingUnclosedBlock + useStreamingArtifactSync), and renders InlineArtifactCards below the message.
- `apps/web/features/chat/utils/artifact-detector.ts:1-63`: Web-local thin wrapper around @agiworkforce/artifacts: extractArtifacts, removeArtifactBlocks, hasArtifacts, extractCodeBlocks/extractArtifactTitle re-export, shouldRenderAsArtifact = isRenderableArtifact.
- `packages/platform/artifacts/src/artifact-derivation.ts:1-260`: Canonical cross-surface (web/desktop/mobile) fence-excision + derivation module: extractCodeBlocks (regex fence scan), isRenderableArtifact, detectArtifactType, extractArtifactTitle, computeDerivedArtifactId (uuidv5), extractTrailingUnclosedBlock (streaming), deriveArtifacts.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/InlineArtifactCards.tsx:1-388`: Inline cards under a message: up to 3 full-width preview/icon cards (+overflow row), a distinct full-diagram card for mermaid, and a 'Download all' button for 2+ artifacts. Clicking a card selects the artifact and opens the side panel.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/ArtifactsPanel.tsx:1-573`: The artifact side panel itself: resizable/collapsible split pane on desktop, full-screen modal overlay on mobile (<640px), tab strip across open artifacts + a live streaming tab, header (title, count badge, cloud-sync status, 'Not saved' quota warning, Download-all, Close), deep-link (`?artifact=`) support, keyboard-shortcut toggle button.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/ArtifactPreview.tsx:1-1916`: The artifact viewer: renders panel-variant toolbar (Preview/Source toggle, version chip+Restore, Edit/Save/Cancel source, Copy, Download dropdown, Publish, Refresh, Open-in-new-tab, Fullscreen, Close) and the card-variant (inline, no version UI). Owns all per-type preview bodies (sandboxed iframe for html/react/svg, Mermaid, spreadsheet/presentation/email/chart via shared renderers, Markdown, PDF, DOCX via mammoth, image) and every empty/error/loading state for each.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/StreamingArtifactView.tsx:1-73`: Live 'Writing…' view shown in the panel while a renderable fence is still open: auto-scrolling monospace body with a blinking caret, sticks to bottom unless the user scrolls up.
- `apps/web/features/chat/stores/artifacts-store.ts:1-200+`: Web-local Zustand artifact store: wraps the shared vanilla @agiworkforce/artifacts store, merges cloud-synced copies, tracks per-artifact version history, panel open/selected state, persistence-degraded and cloud-sync status.
- `apps/web/features/chat/stores/streaming-artifact-store.ts`: Ephemeral store for the single in-flight streaming artifact (id, conversationId, title, content, type/language); auto-opens the panel.
- `apps/web/features/chat/hooks/use-streaming-artifact.ts`: useStreamingArtifactSync, mirrors the trailing unclosed fence into streaming-artifact-store on every chunk.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/publishArtifactClient.ts:1-96`: createWebCloudPublisher, POSTs to /api/artifacts/publish with CSRF header; resolvePublishableKind maps artifact type/language to a publishable kind or throws ArtifactNotPublishableError.
- `apps/web/app/api/artifacts/publish/route.ts:1-100+`: POST handler: CSRF + rate-limit (share-create) + external-sharing gate + secret scan/redaction, then publishArtifactRecord.
- `apps/web/app/api/artifacts/publish/[token]/route.ts`: GET/DELETE for a single published artifact by token (fetch + unpublish).
- `apps/web/app/shared-artifact/[token]/page.tsx:1-62`: Public share route: validates token shape, loads the record, renders PublishedArtifactView or UnavailableArtifact; sets per-artifact <title> metadata, noindex.
- `apps/web/app/shared-artifact/[token]/PublishedArtifactView.tsx:1-119`: Public viewer: sandboxed iframe for scripted kinds (html/react/svg-as-html/mermaid), inert <img> for svg, MarkdownContent for markdown, <pre><code> for code/text; render-error banner.
- `apps/web/app/shared-artifact/[token]/UnavailableArtifact.tsx:1-42`: Expired/revoked/malformed-token state, deliberately not the generic 404.
- `apps/web/lib/services/published-artifact-service.ts:1-404`: Server-side publish/unpublish/list/get for the published_artifacts table (Neon), token generation and validation regex, quota + ownership errors, requiresSandboxedRender.
- `apps/web/features/settings/sections/PublishedArtifactsSection.tsx:1-276`: Settings > Shared links: lists every published artifact for the account (date, size, copy link, Unpublish with confirm dialog), the only place a published page can be taken down, since links have no TTL.
- `apps/web/app/chat/library/page.tsx:1-22`: Library page route: WebAppShell + LibraryView.
- `apps/web/features/library/components/LibraryView.tsx:1-73`: Web LibraryTransport adapter (fetch /api/library, /api/media, exportDocument) wired into the shared LibraryView.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/library/LibraryView.tsx:1-919`: Shared Library/gallery surface: search box, Surface/Origin/Kind filter chips + 'Recently deleted' toggle, card grid (GeneratedFileCard, inline ArtifactRenderer for artifact-surface rows), offset paging ('Show more'), soft-delete/restore/permanent-delete flows with per-item error+retry.
- `apps/web/app/chat/artifacts/page.tsx:1-23`: Retired route: redirects to /chat/library?surface=artifact (Library's own 'Artifacts' filter chip replaced a separate gallery destination).
- `apps/web/app/api/library/route.ts`: GET listing endpoint backing the Library grid (media_assets), origin/kind/surface filtering + paging.
- `apps/web/features/notebook/NotebookPanel.tsx:1-189`: Jupyter-like cell notebook for the Code sandbox surface (/code, CloudCodePage): add/run/run-all cells, per-cell language select, sandbox file list + upload/download. Not the artifact panel, a separate 'document/compute session' editing surface.
- `apps/web/features/notebook/NotebookCellView.tsx:1-160`: One cell: language select, Run/Delete, textarea (Shift+Enter run+advance, Cmd/Ctrl+Enter run in place), output rendering (image/html-table/stream/error) via the shared Spinner primitive while running.
- `apps/web/features/notebook/services/notebook-api.ts`: Client for the code-session notebook backend (list/run/upload/download).
- `apps/web/app/api/code/sessions/[sessionId]/notebook`: Server routes backing NotebookPanel's cell execution and file listing.
