### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:157-213, selector [data-chat-theme='cool'] opt-in`: A ChatGPT-style 'cool' blue-accent palette (--chat-accent-primary:#0b84ff, --chat-text-primary:#0d0d0d) as an alternate to the default warm/amber Claude-style theme (--chat-accent-primary:#da7756), an explicit opt-in data attribute, not automatic vendor detection.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/ArtifactsPanel.tsx vs packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ArtifactPanel.tsx`: Web ships its own bespoke panel/preview/store implementation; desktop uses the shared unified-chat ArtifactPanel + its own ArtifactCategoryFilter/ArtifactVersionHistory/ShareArtifactDialog. Not a runtime branch, two parallel implementations selected at the app level.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/publishedArtifactRender.ts isSandboxedPublishedKind()`: Whether a published-artifact kind (html/react/mermaid vs svg/markdown/code/text) is rendered through the cross-origin sandbox iframe or inertly, the actual security boundary for the public share page.

### Generic dashboard smells

- None observed as a generic-dashboard smell in the strict sense, the panel, cards, and Library all key off real per-artifact/per-file state (title, type, version count, cloud-sync status, generated-file manifest) rather than static placeholder chrome.
- The card-variant toolbar comment at ArtifactPreview.tsx:~1640 explicitly documents and removes a prior fake 'v1/Initial version' history badge that was synthesized for every artifact regardless of real edits, a since-fixed instance of decorative-but-meaningless state worth flagging as the closest thing to a smell in this area's history.

- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/artifact-components/ArtifactSandboxFrame.tsx exists alongside apps/web's own SandboxedIframe.tsx, not confirmed which (if either) apps/web actually renders through; ArtifactPreview.tsx imports the web-local SandboxedIframe, so ArtifactSandboxFrame looks unified-chat/desktop-only, but this wasn't traced further.
- The exact CSS module rules in apps/web/features/notebook/NotebookPanel.module.css (hover/focus/disabled colors) were not read, states there are confirmed only from the TSX/className usage, not the resolved token values.
- hasGeneratedFileManifest / GeneratedFileCard privacy-label rendering (Shield icon flows for tool-generated files attached to an artifact) was read only at the call-site level in ArtifactPreview.tsx, not inside GeneratedFileCard itself.

## Models

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:1-1217`: THE production model picker mounted on the real web chat surface: popover trigger button, model list (Available / More models), reasoning-effort slider, provider logos, tier/env/coming-soon locks, free-lane locked slot. Mounted at ChatComposerNew.tsx:4067-4072 with showModelSelector, lockModelSelector={false}.
- `apps/web/shared/stores/model-store.ts:1-360`: apps/web's own model catalog + Zustand store. AVAILABLE_MODELS is built once at module load via buildAvailableModels(): getAutoRoutingProfiles() entries first, then getModelsForTierAndSurface('max','web/cloud-chat',{modelTypes:[chat,code,reasoning,multimodal,search]}) filtered through getModelMetadata/isCurrentModel, then coming_soon rows from getDisplayModels(). Exposes selectedModelId / setSelectedModelId / getSelectedModel. This is the real data source for the web picker, NOT packages/ui/unified-chat's modelStore.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:2317-2327`: Renders the resolved-model 'served by' footer under each completed assistant message: a small opacity-0→100-on-hover/focus text line reading message.model ?? message.metadata?.model through getManagedModelPresentationLabel(), with a freePool suffix from isFreeRouteLane(message.metadata?.routeLane).
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:2798-2806, 3018, 3362`: Client streaming hook: reads the X-AGI-Resolved-Model response header and writes the resolved model onto the assistant message for the streaming path, the queued/replay path, and the approval-turn path.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/route.ts:488, 596`: Server route: after resolving the model (incl. Auto routing via resolveAutoRoute + classifyTaskLocally, and fallback), sets the X-AGI-Resolved-Model header on both the research-loop and normal response paths.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/approve/route.ts:369`: Sets X-AGI-Resolved-Model on the tool-approval continuation response stream.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/stream-transform.ts:758, 1113`: Streaming transform layer: sets X-AGI-Resolved-Model (from modelUsed) on two stream response shapes (Anthropic-style and OpenAI-style).
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/response-builder.ts:270-315`: Non-streaming JSON response: emits x_agi_workforce.routing (task_type, task_confidence, resolved_model, slot, quota_warning) alongside the OpenAI-shaped completion body, a second, richer transparency channel than the header alone.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:1399 (resolveAutoRoute call), 2257-2266 (classifyTaskLocally)`: Server-side Auto-routing resolution: classifyTaskLocally() + resolveAutoRoute() run here, per request, not client-side. Also holds the provider-specific request-shaping branches (effort path, thinking budget, tool schema) audited below.
- `apps/web/lib/cors.ts:87`: CORS allow-list that exposes X-AGI-Resolved-Model (plus X-AGI-Route-Lane, X-AGI-Fallback-Reason, X-Quota-Warning) to the browser.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/routeLane.ts:1-40`: Reads the X-AGI-Route-Lane response header (ROUTE_LANE_HEADER) client-side and exposes isFreeRouteLane(), consumed by useChatStream.ts to stamp message.routeLane and by MessageBubble.tsx to render the '· via free pool' suffix.
- `packages/contracts/types/src/model-catalog.ts:46-61 (ModelCapabilities), 1466 (getModels), 1742-1836 (reasoning/effort), 633-663 (getAutoRoutingProfiles), 2083 (getPickerModelTier)`: Canonical model catalog types + query API: ModelCapabilities (streaming/tools/vision/json/thinking/computerUse/agentic/imageGen/videoGen/search/research/codeExecution/caching), getModels(), getModelReasoning(), getModelEffortOptions(), resolveModelEffort(), splitEffortsByEntitlement(), getReasoningDepthIndicator(), getPickerModelTier(), getAutoRoutingProfiles(), isAutoModeModelId(). Generated from packages/ai/model-registry/catalog via models.json.
- `packages/ai/model-registry/catalog/routing-policies.json:auto.aliases block`: Source of the Auto alias roster. Only one alias is selectable=true: {id:'auto', profile:'balanced', label:'Auto', description:'Automatically routes each message to the best model for the task, your plan, and cost'}. auto-economy/auto-balanced/auto-premium exist but selectable:false, so getAutoRoutingProfiles() returns exactly one entry.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ModelSelector.tsx:1-928`: A SECOND, fully-built model-picker component in the shared UI package (provider-grouped popover, 'Best (auto)' hardcoded label, ThinkingToggle, Sparkles routing-decision badge, capability chips, loading/error/empty states with data-testid hooks). Verified NOT imported anywhere under apps/web/features/chat (grep for ModelSelector/ChatInput imports there returns nothing); used by apps/desktop/src/App.tsx.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/AgentControl.tsx:1-290`: Shared package's Mode+Effort chip control (ask/auto/plan/bypass mode, granular Effort slider), backed by agentControlStore. Rendered via ChatInput.tsx, which is likewise not mounted in apps/web/features/chat.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ProvenanceFooter.tsx:1-102`: Shared package's richer per-message footer: model · provider · N tools · N citations · relative time, plus an 'Auto routed: task → model · Why? "reason"' line with a Pin-to-model button when routing.source==='auto'. Rendered by MessageList.tsx (packages/ui), which apps/web's chat page does not mount, apps/web uses its own MessageBubble.tsx footer instead (see above), which has no task label, no 'Why?' reason, and no Pin button.

### States covered

default (unselected row, hover:bg-muted/60); hover (ModelRow hover:bg-muted/40..60, footer text opacity-0→100); focus (group-focus-within reveal on MessageBubble footer; focus-visible ring on the effort Slider thumb); active/selected (isSelected → bg-muted/40, Check icon, font-medium text-foreground); disabled/locked-tier (opacity-80, Upgrade/Pro badge, still clickable → opens upgrade dialog); disabled/env-locked (opacity-80, cursor-not-allowed, Beta badge, hard-disabled button); disabled/coming_soon (opacity-45, cursor-not-allowed, 'Coming soon' badge, hard-disabled); loading (modelChangePending → trigger shows 'Saving…' and is disabled); streaming (MessageBubble footer suppressed: message.isStreaming shows a blank space instead of the model label); success (resolved model differs from requested → message.model updated, footer shows it); failure (onModelChange rejects → toast 'Could not save the model for this conversation…', previous model remains); empty (recommended.length===0 && isSearching → 'No models match'); overflow (model name truncate + min-w floor/max-w cap so trigger never overflows past Send at narrow widths); mobile (StyleSelector hidden below sm breakpoint; composer row stays single-line)

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ModelSelector.tsx:516, 519`: Splits displayModels into autoModels (provider==='managed_cloud' or isAutoModeModelId) vs providerModels for separate rendering sections in the shared-package picker (not used by apps/web's chat page).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:100 (toProviderId)`: providerKey==='managed_cloud' maps to the 'agi-cloud' ProviderId for PROVIDER_DISPLAY lookup; otherwise passes the key through only if it exists in PROVIDER_DISPLAY, else null (no icon).
- `ComposerFooter.tsx:355-357 (partitionModels isAuto)`: m.providerKey==='managed_cloud' identifies the Auto row(s) so they sort first in both the Available and More-models groups.
- `ComposerFooter.tsx:369-410 (ProviderLogo)`: providerKey==='managed_cloud' renders the AgiMark brand mark; otherwise tries ProviderMark (hasProviderMark), then a local /providers/<id>.svg <img>, then a plain brand-color dot as last resort.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/ProviderMark.tsx:29 (hasProviderMark) and :44 (component)`: key==='openai' renders a hardcoded inline OpenAI SVG path (not in the simple-icons ICON_BY_PROVIDER map); every other key looks up ICON_BY_PROVIDER (siClaude for anthropic/claude, siGooglegemini for google/gemini, siDeepseek, siMoonshotai for moonshot/kimi, siOllama, siPerplexity, siQwen, siMistralai, siX for xai/grok); an unmapped key renders nothing.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:798`: modelSupportsEffort() fallback (when the model isn't in the catalog): provider==='anthropic'\|\|'openai'\|\|'google' → true, else false, gates whether an effort value is forwarded at all.
- `request-processor.ts:869-872`: provider==='openai' additionally requires supportsOpenAIReasoningEffort() before an effort value survives, OpenAI-only extra gate on top of the generic capability check.
- `request-processor.ts:879`: anthropicUsesAdaptiveThinking(): provider!=='anthropic' short-circuits to false, adaptive-thinking budgeting logic is Anthropic-only.
- `request-processor.ts:914`: provider!=='anthropic' returns undefined from the thinking-budget builder, non-Anthropic requests never get a {type,budget_tokens} thinking block.
- `request-processor.ts:1206`: modelHasNativeAnthropicWebFetch(): provider==='anthropic' && providerCompatibility.nativeWebFetch!==false gates whether the native web_fetch tool is offered vs a generic fetch tool.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/canonical-request.ts:56, 68, 106, 121, 130, 188`: Provider-specific request-shape branches: openrouter/open_router model-id passthrough, anthropic-only fields (x2), google-only field, openai-only field, pure request-body construction, not UI.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/context-compaction.ts:125; app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/lib/generate-title.ts:168; lib/support/agent/answer/model-route.ts:76; lib/services/scheduled-agent-executor.ts:334`: Four independent copies of the same check, route.provider==='anthropic'\|\|'google', gating which routes are eligible for a given backend feature (context compaction, title generation, support-agent answers, scheduled-agent execution), a repeated literal rather than a shared helper.
- `apps/web/lib/cost-tracker.ts:55; apps/web/lib/prompt-cache-helper.ts:36`: meta.provider==='anthropic' selects Anthropic's distinct cache-token accounting formula (input+cacheRead+cacheWrite vs plain input) for cost/usage math, backend billing logic, not rendering.
- `apps/web/lib/server/container-files.ts:155-158`: ref.provider==='openai' vs 'anthropic' selects which provider's file-reference resolution path to use for container-generated files.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/cards/MapSearchCard.tsx:280`: action.provider==='google_maps' selects the primary action button among a search result's actions, the one UI-rendering branch on a vendor value in a message card (falls back to actions[0]).
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:667`: Validates a persisted video job's provider is one of 'google'\|'runway'\|'openrouter' before restoring it into message metadata on reload, a data-shape guard, not a rendering fork.
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useMediaGeneration.ts:299, 362, 365-367`: Image/video generation polling hook: validates and normalizes provider ('open_router'→'openrouter' display key) to decide which poll/complete branch and provider label to show while generation is in-flight, feeds UI state (progress/labels) though the branch itself is a data hook, not JSX.
- `apps/web/app/api/media/image/generate/route.ts:249-350, 1386 (switch); apps/web/app/api/media/video/generate/route.ts:310-1294; apps/web/lib/services/video-provider-output-service.ts (multiple); apps/web/lib/services/media-model-availability-service.ts:55-94`: A large family of provider==='openai'\|'google'\|'runway'\|'openrouter' branches selecting request shape, polling endpoint, and pricing/duration formula for image/video generation, entirely server-side route/service logic, not UI rendering.

### Generic dashboard smells

- Two independently maintained model-picker stacks exist in the codebase (apps/web's ComposerFooter+model-store.ts vs packages/ui's ModelSelector+modelStore.ts) with different Auto labels ('Auto' vs 'Best (auto)'), different disabled-during-streaming behavior, and different served-by footers (plain text vs full provenance+Pin), a change made in one does not propagate to the other, and nothing enforces they stay in sync.
- The same `route.provider === 'anthropic' \|\| route.provider === 'google'` gate is copy-pasted in at least four unrelated backend files (context-compaction, generate-title, support-agent model-route, scheduled-agent-executor) instead of a shared helper, a change to which providers qualify requires editing all four.
- packages/ui/unified-chat's rich Auto-routing transparency (task label, 'Why?' reason, Pin-to-model) is fully built and tested but has no consumer on the actual web chat surface, so the effort invested there delivers no visible transparency to web users today.
- ComposerFooter.tsx (1217 lines) mixes data derivation (partitionModels, modelLock, reasoning capability), presentational subcomponents (ModelRow, ProviderLogo), and the top-level popover/effort-slider markup in one file with no internal module boundaries.

### Open questions

- Is packages/ui/unified-chat's ModelSelector/AgentControl/ProvenanceFooter stack intended as the future replacement for apps/web's ComposerFooter, or a permanently separate desktop/mobile implementation? No shared abstraction currently keeps their Auto label, streaming-disable behavior, or served-by footer content in sync.
- apps/web/shared/stores/model-store.ts:192-197 hardcodes getAllowedAutoModesForTier() to always return ['auto'] regardless of tier, is that intentional (the catalog only ever exposes one selectable Auto alias today) or a placeholder for the auto-economy/auto-balanced/auto-premium aliases that exist in routing-policies.json but are marked selectable:false?
- The x_agi_workforce.routing block in response-builder.ts (task_type/task_confidence/resolved_model/slot) is emitted on the OpenAI-compatible /v1 API but not read or displayed anywhere in the chat UI found, confirm whether any consumer (dashboards, API users) actually reads it, or whether it is dead transparency.
- ComposerFooter's model list has no client-visible loading/error state because AVAILABLE_MODELS is synchronous and static; confirm this is acceptable given packages/ai/model-registry catalog updates require a deploy (not a runtime fetch) to reach the picker.

## Files

### Entry points

- `apps/web/app/api/uploads/presign/route.ts:66-251`: POST issues a presigned/proxy PUT URL for kind avatar\|knowledge-file\|chat-attachment after validateAttachmentMeta plus per-kind size/type checks; picks same-origin proxy vs direct R2 PUT depending on CORS-safe dev origin. DELETE cleans up an abandoned knowledge-file object.
- `apps/web/app/api/uploads/chat-attachment/put/route.ts:25-66`: Same-origin PUT proxy target for chat attachments when direct R2 CORS is unavailable; enforces owned-key prefix and MAX_CHAT_ATTACHMENT_BYTES on both declared Content-Length and actual body size.
- `apps/web/app/api/uploads/chat-attachment/complete/route.ts:49-211`: POST after the PUT: re-validates type, dedupes on existing media_asset, bounds-reads the stored object, checks hash denylist, runs scanUploadBytes content inspection, atomically seals scanned bytes under a new key (copyPrivateObjectIfUnchanged by ETag), purges the pre-scan key, and inserts the media_asset row. Returns {id, name, mimeType, byteCount, type, url:'/api/files/{id}'}.
- `apps/web/app/api/uploads/knowledge-file/put/route.ts:23-80`: Same-origin PUT proxy for project knowledge files (mirrors chat-attachment/put but validates the key against the project + MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES=25MiB).
- `apps/web/app/api/uploads/local-project-knowledge/route.ts:38-57`: Development-only local-disk substitute for R2 when no object storage is configured; gated to NODE_ENV=development, 404s otherwise.
- `apps/web/app/api/projects/[id]/knowledge-files/route.ts:1-60 (file top; full route continues)`: POST registers/extracts a project knowledge file (validateAttachmentMeta, MAX_KNOWLEDGE_FILES=20, free-plan storage-byte entitlement, scanUploadBytes via project-knowledge-extraction, moderation event on rejection); GET lists files + storage usage.
- `apps/web/app/api/projects/[id]/knowledge-files/[fileId]/route.ts:n/a - not opened in full, referenced from KnowledgeFilesPanel/SourcesPanel delete calls`: DELETE removes one project knowledge file (row + storage object).
- `apps/web/app/api/files/[id]/route.ts:66-198`: Authenticated same-origin byte-serving route behind every attachment/generated-file URL (/api/files/{id}). UUID-validates id, RLS-scoped asset lookup, 30MiB inline cap (256MiB for video with HTTP Range support), forces Content-Disposition/nosniff via servedByteHeaders, adds AI-Act provenance headers, private no-store cache.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:1217 (fileInputRef), 1394-1541 (paste/drop handlers), 2063-2280 (handleSubmit assembling sendArgs), 2665 (DragDropOverlay mount), 3255-3283 (menu row), 4245-4264 (hidden input), 2876-2935 (model-conflict banner)`: Composer: owns the hidden file input, the '+' overflow menu's 'Add photos & files' row, handlePaste/handleEditorPasteDecision, handleFileDrop, screenshot capture, model-capability conflict banner, and assembles attachments into the onSend() call.
- `apps/web/features/chat/hooks/use-attachments.ts:82-183`: Client-side attachment state: addFiles/removeFile/clearAll, per-file size (12MiB) and MIME/extension gating via isSupportedChatAttachment, object-URL preview lifecycle with revoke-on-unmount.
- `apps/web/features/chat/services/chat-attachment-upload.ts:7-25`: Wraps createManagedCloudChatAttachmentsClient with a CSRF header decorator; converts the client's ManagedCloudChatAttachment[] into the app's Attachment shape.
- `packages/contracts/cloud-contracts/src/managed-cloud-chat-attachments-client.ts:94-174`: Shared (web + other surfaces) presign -> PUT -> complete upload orchestration; enforces MAX_CHAT_ATTACHMENT_COUNT=10 and an aggregate MAX_CHAT_ATTACHMENT_BYTES=12MiB across the whole file[] before any network call; refuses a non-https, non-same-origin upload URL.
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:1665-1706`: Calls uploadChatAttachments(files) to turn File[] into durable Attachment[] before invoking sendMessage(); on failure parks the draft + restores attachments via droppedFiles prop.
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:2456-2560, 2686`: sendMessage(): stamps the optimistic user Message with attachments + a durable-only attachments descriptor in metadata (durableAttachmentDescriptors), and later serializes history turns via buildApiMessageContent for the outbound request.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/persisted-attachments.ts:52-66`: buildApiMessageContent(message) turns an Attachment[] into OpenAI-shaped content parts: {type:'file',file:{asset_id}} for durable assets, {type:'image_url',image_url:{url}} for inline/base64 image content.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/chat-attachment-hydration.ts:271-457`: Server-side hydration: resolves every content part's asset_id to real bytes, enforces MAX_REQUEST_ATTACHMENT_COUNT=20 and MAX_REQUEST_ATTACHMENT_BYTES=18MiB (current-turn files charged first), degrades historical-turn failures to an inline text note instead of failing the request, throws ChatAttachmentHydrationError (with HTTP status) only for the live/current turn's own attachments.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:170, 1993-2010, 2126-2260`: Calls hydrateChatAttachments(chatRequest.messages, userId) before building the provider call and maps ChatAttachmentHydrationError to a client-facing error response; also validates/filters raw image_url parts elsewhere in the route (egress policy, data-URI mime sniff).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:955-1170 (generated-file assembly), 1819-1937 (attachment grid + lightbox)`: Renders both user-sent attachments (message.attachments) and assistant tool/provider-generated files (metadata.generatedFiles) through one shared attachment grid; image lightbox; broken-image fallback; download/preview chips for docs.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/DragDropOverlay.tsx:15-141`: Window-level dragenter/dragover/drop listeners (works regardless of drop target inside the page) rendering a full-screen dashed-border overlay while a file drag is in progress.
- `packages/platform/utils/src/composerPaste.ts:38-105`: Framework-neutral paste/drop decision shared by web, unified-chat, and the Chrome extension: decideComposerPaste() classifies a DataTransfer into files \| large-text-as-attachment \| plain text.
- `apps/web/features/projects/components/KnowledgeFilesPanel.tsx:43-451`: Compact knowledge-files list + uploader rendered inside ProjectSettingsDialog (a Radix Dialog portaled to document.body); dropzone, per-account storage meter, MAX_PROJECT_KNOWLEDGE_FILES=20 cap messaging, useConfirmAction-gated delete.
- `apps/web/features/projects/components/SourcesPanel.tsx:1-604`: Primary/live knowledge-files surface on the project detail page itself (apps/web/app/chat/projects/[id]/page.tsx:782); sort order, HardDrive/MessageSquare iconography, delegates upload UI to AddSourcesModal.
- `apps/web/features/projects/components/AddSourcesModal.tsx:67-557`: Modal for adding project sources: file picker, window-level DropOverlay, and paste-text-as-source (onUploadText).
- `apps/web/features/projects/components/FilePreviewModal.tsx:179-394`: Shared preview modal (used by both KnowledgeFilesPanel and SourcesPanel) for image/PDF-iframe/markdown/syntax-highlighted-code/plain-text/unsupported-type previews, plus a Download button that blob-fetches then triggers an anchor click.
- `apps/web/features/projects/services/project-knowledge-upload.ts:33-43`: Thin wrapper over createManagedCloudProjectKnowledgeClient; onProgress is called only at 0 and 100 (no real upload-progress events).

### States covered

default (empty composer, empty knowledge list); hover (RemoveButton, attachment chips, list rows - Tailwind hover: classes); focus (RemoveButton and lightbox controls use focus-visible:ring; file rows are keyboard-activatable via onKeyDown Enter/Space); active/pressed (button press states via Tailwind, not separately verified per element); disabled (composerDisabled gates the hidden file input and the 'Add photos & files' menu row; upload button disabled while isUploading); loading (KnowledgeFilesPanel 'Loading...' text state; SourcesPanel has a 15s load timeout with its own error path; upload spinner text 'Uploading "name"... N%'); streaming (not directly applicable to attachments themselves, but generated-file text content streams in via a separate fetch effect while the assistant message may still be isStreaming); success (upload completes -> file appears in list / chip appears in composer; ImageLightbox 'ready' render); failure (upload error banner in KnowledgeFilesPanel with Dismiss; toast on knowledge-file delete failure with optimistic-state rollback; ChatAttachmentHydrationError surfaced as a chat error; broken-image fallback chip; PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE 413 from /api/files/[id]); empty (KnowledgeFilesPanel's dedicated empty-state dropzone copy 'No knowledge files yet'; composer with no attachments renders nothing - AttachmentPreview returns null); overflow (long filenames truncated with ellipsis and a title tooltip in both AttachmentPreview's DocumentChip and MessageBubble's file chip; knowledge-files list scroll not specially handled beyond normal flow); mobile (composer's overflow-menu 'Add attachments and tools' button is exercised by ChatComposerNew.mobileFooter.test.tsx; no dedicated mobile-only attachment grid/thumbnail sizing was found - AttachmentPreview and the message-bubble grid rely on flex-wrap reflow rather than a responsive breakpoint variant)

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ai/provider-runtime/src/history.ts:33,108-113,211,235 (policy: 'anthropic-shape' | 'openai-shape')`: How image/document/file content blocks in conversation history are shaped before being handed to a provider adapter; a media-pruning routine (line ~161-208) also drops the oldest image/document/pdf blocks first when trimming history for context length, before dropping text.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/chat-attachment-hydration.ts (isChatImageMimeType branch at line 386 vs. generic 'file' branch at 419-444)`: Whether a resolved attachment becomes an image_url data-URI part (base64) or a file/file_data part with a mime_type - this shape is provider-family-agnostic at this layer; the anthropic-shape/openai-shape translation happens further downstream in packages/ai.

### Generic dashboard smells

- KnowledgeFilesPanel and FilePreviewModal style themselves entirely with inline style={{...}} objects referencing var(--agi-\*) design tokens that a repo comment says exist specifically NOT to bleed into /chat product UI, yet both components render inside a Radix Dialog (ProjectSettingsDialog) whose Portal targets document.body by default with no container override found in packages/ui/ui/src/primitives/Dialog.tsx - meaning these tokens are likely undefined in that render context (see openQuestions).
- Two independently-implemented knowledge-file list/upload UIs (SourcesPanel and KnowledgeFilesPanel) exist for the same project resource with separate React state and no observed shared cache, which is the kind of duplicated-surface pattern that drifts silently over time.
- uploadProjectKnowledgeFile's onProgress callback is a UI-shaped API (percentage) wired to a UI that displays a live percentage, but the implementation only ever calls it with 0 or 100 - the granularity promised by the interface is not delivered.
- The client aggregate-byte guard in managed-cloud-chat-attachments-client.ts reuses the exact same constant as the single-file cap, making the 'up to 10 files' promise largely unreachable in practice for anything but very small files, and the resulting failure surfaces only after the tray already accepted the files.

### Tests

- apps/web/app/api/uploads/**tests**/chat-attachment-moderation.test.ts - hash-denylist accept/reject, ETag-changed-between-scan-and-seal rejection, byte-count-overrun rejection, ok-with-no-denylist-configured
- apps/web/app/api/uploads/**tests**/presign-upload-policy.test.ts - per-kind type/size policy incl. avatar cap vs attachment cap, PDF knowledge-file private-bucket-only signing, same-origin proxy routing off the CORS-safe dev origin, cross-workspace project rejection
- apps/web/features/chat/lib/persisted-attachments.test.ts
- apps/web/features/chat/services/chat-attachment-upload.test.ts
- apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/AttachmentPreview.test.tsx

- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/**tests**/AttachmentMenu.folderSelect.test.tsx

### Open questions

- Does document.body (or any ancestor of the Radix Dialog portal root) ever carry data-design="agi" at runtime? This pass found data-design="agi" applied only to specific content divs inside apps/web/app/chat/projects/[id]/page.tsx (lines 208/229/267/316) and apps/web/app/chat/projects/page.tsx:145, never on <html>/<body> or in any layout.tsx, and Dialog.tsx's DialogPrimitive.Portal is used with no container prop - if that holds, every var(--agi-\*) reference in KnowledgeFilesPanel.tsx and FilePreviewModal.tsx resolves to nothing (falls back to inherited/initial), which would need a live DOM inspection (devtools computed-style) to confirm, not just static analysis.
- Which of SourcesPanel vs. KnowledgeFilesPanel is the intended canonical knowledge-files surface, and is there a plan to consolidate them? Not resolved from code alone.
- The full per-provider translation of image_url/file parts (Anthropic content blocks, Gemini inlineData, etc.) was only traced to the anthropic-shape/openai-shape policy switch in packages/ai/provider-runtime/src/history.ts; the individual adapter files (packages/ai/providers/openai/src/translate.ts, translate-responses.ts) were located but not read in this pass.
- Whether the 12 MiB per-file / 12 MiB aggregate mismatch in the chat-attachment upload client is a known, accepted limitation (effectively capping useful batches to 1-2 files) or an unnoticed bug - no comment in managed-cloud-chat-attachments-client.ts addresses why the aggregate cap equals the per-file cap rather than e.g. 12 MiB x 10.
- apps/web/app/api/projects/[id]/knowledge-files/[fileId]/route.ts (the delete endpoint) was located via grep/import references but its body was not read in this pass - only inferred from its callers.

## Connectors

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/settings/components/WebSettingsModal.tsx:1218-1338`: Web's settings-modal wrapper. Builds `sectionContent` for the 18 content sections and a `directoryAdapter` (via useDirectoryAdapter) that drives the built-in Skills/Connectors/Plugins panes; wires renderConnectorDetailFooter, renderConnectorScopes, renderConnectorCapabilities, renderConnectorToolPermissions, connectorDisclosure into <SettingsModal>.
- `apps/web/features/settings/lib/web-settings-sections.ts:18-58`: Declares WEB_SETTINGS_CONTENT_SECTIONS (18 keys with sectionContent entries) and WEB_SETTINGS_BUILT_IN_SECTIONS = ['connectors','skills','plugins'] rendered by the shared shell from the adapter, not from sectionContent.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/settings-nav.ts:254-280`: SETTINGS_NAV_GROUPS_WEB: the single-group, 17-item web nav list (general, account, team, privacy, billing, usage, capabilities, security, safety, notifications, reflect, time-focus, skills, connectors, plugins, help, 'archived'/'deleted-chats'/'shared-links' are routable but excluded from the rail).
- `packages/ui/ui/src/settings-modal/SettingsModal.tsx:58-61, 1897-1955`: Shared modal shell: renderSection() branches to DirectoryPanel whenever a directoryAdapter is supplied and covers the active section (always true for web, since useDirectoryAdapter's `sections` is a hardcoded constant of all three), otherwise falls back to legacy in-file ConnectorsPanel/SkillsPanel/PluginsPanel table views (desktop's path, since apps/desktop's <SettingsModal> passes no directoryAdapter).
- `apps/web/features/directory/hooks/useDirectoryAdapter.ts:70-443`: Builds the DirectoryAdapter web actually uses: load/install/uninstall/openSettings/createEntry/copyLink/openHref/addMarketplace/removeMarketplace for all three sections, backed by the three services below.
- `apps/web/features/directory/services/{connectors,skills,plugins}-directory.ts`: Fetch + shape API responses into DirectoryEntry/DirectorySection/DirectoryDetail; own all copy, facet, badge and sort logic per section.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/directory/DirectoryPanel.tsx`: The actual rendered UI for Skills/Connectors/Plugins on web: toolbar, Installed/Popular/Catalog grid split, install-confirm flow, marketplace dialog, and detail-view routing.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/directory/{DirectoryGrid,DirectoryToolbar,ConnectorDetailView,SkillDetailView,PluginDetailView,DirectoryDetailHeader,AddMarketplaceDialog,SkillFileViewer}.tsx`: Presentational pieces of the directory (cards, search/filter/sort bar, per-kind detail panes, file viewer).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:709-900, 1752-1900, 2770-2807, 3567-3691`: Composer: plus-menu Skills/Connectors/Plugins rows, the Connectors submenu (per-conversation enable/disable), the selectedSkillName pill, the selectedMcpContext (prompt/resource) pill, and the @-mention / slash-command wiring for skills.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/SlashCommandMenu.tsx`: Web wrapper that builds the suggestion list (built-ins + custom commands + skills) fed to the shared menu component.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/SlashCommandMenu.tsx`: Presentational slash-command listbox (renders nothing when suggestions.length === 0, no explicit loading/error row).
- `apps/web/features/chat/hooks/use-skills-list.ts`: Fetches the installed/'included' skill catalog for @-mention and slash suggestions; exposes loading/error that the menu UI does not consume.
- `apps/web/features/connectors/components/{ConnectorScopeList,ConnectorCapabilitiesPanel,ConnectorConsentSummary,ToolPermissionsPanel}.tsx`: Connector scope-manifest, live-capability, consent-disclosure, and per-tool-permission UIs. Only ToolPermissionsPanel is reachable on web; the other three are wired only into SettingsModal props that feed the dead legacy ConnectorsPanel branch.
- `apps/web/lib/connectors/scope-descriptions.ts`: The scope manifest data: canonical OAuth scope → plain-language sentence + read/write class, keyed off getConnectorScopeCeiling() per connector.

### States covered

default (populated grid); loading (DirectoryGrid spinner on first page; SkillDetailView file spinner; AddMarketplaceDialog submit spinner); error (DirectoryGrid error+retry; SkillDetailView fileError; DirectoryPanel detailError; AddMarketplaceDialog inline submit error); empty (DirectoryGrid 'No X match this search'; AddMarketplaceDialog 'lists no plugins yet'; composer 'No connectors connected yet.'; settings nav search 'No matches.'); hover (hover:bg-muted / hover:border-foreground/20 on cards and rows); focus (DIRECTORY_FOCUS_RING / FOCUS_RING applied to every interactive element in the directory and modal shell); active/pressed (aria-pressed on source chips, aria-expanded on filter/sort menus and the Connectors submenu, aria-checked on ConnectorCheckboxRow, aria-current='page' on the active nav row); disabled (install/remove buttons disabled while entry.mutating or a general busy flag; entries with installable:false simply omit the Add control); loading-in-place / mutating (Spinner swapped in for the trailing icon on a card, and inside the primary detail-header button, while an install/uninstall/connect call is in flight); success/failure per entry (entry.error renders a red text line under the card, driven by connectorReauthorizationErrors / withConnectorErrors); overflow (ChipList 'Show more'/'Show less' at 8 items; truncate/line-clamp-2 on names and descriptions; settings-nav scroll-fade gradients top and bottom); mobile (SettingsModal dialog reflows nav from a 220px left rail to a max-h-64% horizontal top bar; DirectoryGrid drops to grid-cols-1; the legacy (desktop-only-in-practice) table panels hide secondary columns under sm:table-cell)

### Vendor branches

- `apps/web/features/directory/services/connectors-directory.ts:30-41,73-76 (BADGE_TO_KIND, CURATED_BADGE/SELF_ADDED_BADGE, CONNECTABLE_BLOCKED)`: Whether a connector renders as verified/community/'yours', and whether its Add control shows at all (installable:false for connectable modes 'desktop-and-cli' and 'needs-setup', i.e. connectors that require the desktop app or manual setup can't be connected from web).
- `apps/web/lib/connectors/directory/sources/first-party.json + FIRST_PARTY_TARGETS_BY_ID (connectors-directory.ts:43-56)`: For curated (first-party) connectors, supplies the tool-name list, documentation URL and connector URL shown in the detail pane, derived from a static per-connector JSON manifest rather than the live registry record.
- `apps/web/lib/connectors/scope-descriptions.ts:26-260 (SCOPE_DESCRIPTIONS)`: Per-provider (Google Gmail/Calendar/Drive/Sheets/Analytics/YouTube/BigQuery/Cloud-Storage, Microsoft Graph, Meta/Instagram/Facebook Pages, SMART-on-FHIR) canonical OAuth scope strings map to a plain-language sentence and a read/write access class; an unrecognized scope for a known connector falls back to a generic 'This permission has not been described yet.' (classified write, i.e. fails safe toward the stricter badge).
- `apps/web/features/directory/services/skills-directory.ts:38-46 (OWNED_SOURCES, skillPublisher)`: Whether a skill's publisher line reads 'Yours' (personal/project/workspace source), 'Managed' (managed-local), or 'Made by AGI' (anything else).
- `apps/web/features/directory/services/plugins-directory.ts:61,102-105 (firstParty = publisher.kind === 'first-party')`: Whether a plugin files under the 'Made by AGI' or 'Partners' source chip.

### Generic dashboard smells

- ~950 lines of legacy table-based ConnectorsPanel/SkillsPanel/PluginsPanel UI (packages/ui/ui/src/settings-modal/SettingsModal.tsx, roughly lines 750-1690) ship inside the same shared package web imports, but are structurally unreachable from the web app (web's directoryAdapter always covers all three sections, so renderSection() never falls into the branches that mount them) -- effectively desktop-only code carried as dead weight in web's bundle and never exercised by a web test.
- Three purpose-built, non-trivial connector-detail components (ConnectorScopeList, ConnectorCapabilitiesPanel, ConnectorConsentSummary) are fully wired as SettingsModal props (renderConnectorScopes/renderConnectorCapabilities/connectorDisclosure) but those props are only read inside the same dead ConnectorsPanel branch -- so on web none of them ever render, despite each having its own docstring explaining why the surface (not the shell) owns that data.
- DirectoryConnectorDetail.permissions and CONNECTOR_PERMISSIONS_LABEL ('Permissions') exist in the shared directory types/constants (packages/ui/ui/src/directory/types.ts:100, constants.ts:83) but no producer ever populates `permissions` and ConnectorDetailView never renders that label -- a scope/permission summary was designed into the generic directory detail schema and then never connected to the real scope-manifest data (which instead lives in the unreachable ConnectorScopeList).
- generic 'No content for section "{key}".' fallback text (SettingsModal.tsx ~1953) is a leftover debug-string affordance for an un-routed section rather than a designed empty/error state -- the same class of bug the web-settings-sections.ts docstring says it exists to prevent for the _nav_, but the fallback string itself is still present as a last resort.

### Open questions

- Is the legacy ConnectorsPanel/SkillsPanel/PluginsPanel code in packages/ui/ui/src/settings-modal/SettingsModal.tsx still desktop's live UI, or also dead there -- worth confirming with apps/desktop before treating it as removable, since this map only verified apps/web's reachability.
- Was surfacing ConnectorScopeList / ConnectorCapabilitiesPanel / ConnectorConsentSummary on web's connector detail pane (via DirectoryAdapter.renderDetailFooter, the one footer slot web's ConnectorDetailView actually has) deliberately deferred, or is this an unnoticed regression from the DirectoryPanel migration -- the components, their tests, and their SettingsModal prop plumbing are all still present and current, which reads like an incomplete cutover rather than an intentional removal.
- Given ConnectorCapabilitiesPanel is currently the only caller of publishMcpContextSelection, is the composer's resource/prompt-attach chip (selectedMcpContext, ChatComposerNew.tsx:2785-2806) meant to be reachable at all on web today, or is it also mid-migration groundwork.
- Should useSkillsList's loading/error be surfaced in the slash-command menu (currently a fetch failure silently drops the skills section with no error row), or is that considered acceptable because built-in commands still render.
- Is DirectoryConnectorDetail.permissions (never populated) meant to replace or duplicate the separate ConnectorScopeList data path once/if the latter is wired back in -- two schemas for the same concept currently coexist.

## Search

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:1046-1090, 3688-3702, 4477-4499`: Composer: ambient web-search indicator (read-only text, no click) and the Deep Research toggle inside the '+' overflow menu; also gates both on selected-model capabilities.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:1338-1466, 1529-1566, 1746-1752, 2285-2312`: Per-message orchestration: collects search sources/citations from metadata, renders ResearchActivity header, AgentActivityTimeline/ToolTimeline, SourcesControl, the no-results notice, and feeds citations into MarkdownContent.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/research/ResearchPanel.tsx:283-563`: Right-side Sources/Report/Library panel, the compact SourcesControl button, and ResearchToggleButton for the chat header.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/research/ResearchActivity.tsx:1-272`: Live Deep Research status header shown inline in the transcript: phase label, elapsed clock, search/source counts vs caps, plan step list, retry and plan-approval controls.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/research/ResearchReportView.tsx`: Renders a persisted research report (markdown + in-report citation anchors + TOC).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/research/ResearchReportsGallery.tsx`: Library tab: past research reports for the account.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ToolTimeline.tsx:83-137, 366-462`: The general per-tool-call renderer (see Tools), not search-specific or legacy; special-cases the 'Web search' step as a plain label + result count with no request/response box, since a search call has no useful request/response payload to show.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/AgentActivityTimeline.tsx:63-135, 287-349, 386-398`: Canonical agent activity spine used by the production MessageBubble: web-search category summaries, running/'Reading N sources' state, and the SourceLinks list row.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/MarkdownContent.tsx:188-290`: Renders assistant markdown; resolves '#chat-citation-N' / '#chat-citations-N,M' hrefs and bare matching links into CitationChip.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/CitationChip.tsx`: Inline citation chip: favicon + site name (+N for grouped), Radix tooltip listing every grouped source.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/markdown/citationMarkers.ts`: Turns model-emitted [n]/[n][m] marker runs into citation links, fence/inline-code safe, streaming-safe (open marker left as text); also resolves plain inline links to a citation index (claude.ai parity).
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/citation-links.ts`: Separate citation-linking pass for Deep Research report markdown: [n] -> '#research-source-N' anchors, independent of citationMarkers.ts.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/researchReportSources.ts`: Strips a report's trailing duplicate 'Sources:' list and strips a plain chat reply's trailing citation-only block.
- `apps/web/features/chat/utils/research-sources.ts`: De-dupes sources by normalized URL and splits them into 'cited' (referenced by an [n] marker in the current content) vs 'more'.
- `apps/web/features/chat/stores/research-panel-store.ts`: Zustand store holding the active conversation's cited/more sources, panel open state and query, keyed by conversationId/messageId.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/dialogs/GlobalSearchDialog.tsx`: Cmd/Ctrl+K conversation-search dialog: debounced query, role/date/archived filters, recent + popular search suggestions, results list.
- `apps/web/features/chat/services/global-search-service.ts`: Client service wrapping /api/search (search, recent, popular, suggestions, track).
- `apps/web/features/chat/hooks/use-keyboard-shortcuts.ts:23-31, 117-165`: Binds Cmd/Ctrl+K (and other shortcuts) to onSearch etc; driven by KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT_DOCS as the single source for both behavior and the shortcuts-help list.
- `apps/web/shared/components/CommandPalette/CommandPaletteProvider.tsx:11-20`: App-wide Cmd/Ctrl+K command palette; explicitly no-ops on /chat routes to avoid colliding with the chat page's own Cmd+K binding.
- `apps/web/shared/components/CommandPalette/CommandPalette.tsx:86-91`: Command palette UI; its 'Search Conversations' command navigates to /chat?search=true.
- `apps/web/app/api/search/route.ts`: GET: ILIKE search across sessions/projects/media/messages scoped to user+active org, plus type=recent/popular/suggestions sub-routes backed by DB functions. POST: track-search analytics (CSRF + rate-limited).
- `apps/web/app/api/research/reports/route.ts`: GET: fetch one report by requestId, or list reports for a conversation (RLS-scoped db).
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/research-loop.ts`: Server-side Deep Research orchestration: plan -> bounded gathering rounds (web_search/url_fetch) -> cited synthesis; emits x_research_status/x_tool_status/x_search_results SSE deltas.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:1176-1200`: appendWebSearchTool: injects the provider-native search tool (anthropic/google/openai) per turn.
- `packages/ai/search/src/web-search-support.ts`: Registry-derived capability queries: which providers implement native web search / tool injection, and the combined isWebSearchAvailable() decision the composer reads.
- `packages/ai/providers/google/src/stream.ts:9-60, 195-282`: Translates Gemini groundingMetadata (groundingChunks/groundingSupports) and urlContextMetadata into citation-delta and server-tool-result('gemini_grounding_result') stream chunks.
- `packages/ai/provider-protocol/src/openai-wire-compat.ts:279-301, 490-510, 644-660`: Re-shapes every provider's native search payload onto the OpenAI-compatible SSE wire (x_search_results/x_tool_status), applying the shared 5-result render cap.
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:1146-1211, 2020-2210`: Client SSE parser: x_tool_status/x_search_results/citation-delta handling, and the Gemini-specific synthetic 'Searching the web' activity entry (Gemini has no discrete start signal).
- `apps/web/lib/web-search/web-search-tool.ts`: Generic (non-native) web search tool backed by the Perplexity Search API; used when the selected provider has no native search path.

### Behaviours

| Behaviour                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Where                                                                                                                                                                       | Status      | Note                                                                                                             |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Web search is ambient (auto-on when the selected model/deployment has a search path), not a manual toggle -- the toggle button was deliberately removed.                                                                                                                                                       | apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:1073-1090, 4472-4478                                                                                         | implemented |                                                                                                                  |
| Composer shows a read-only 'Web search on'/'Web search off' indicator with an explanatory title tooltip; no click handler.                                                                                                                                                                                     | apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:4488-4500                                                                                                    | implemented | Covered by ChatComposerNew.webSearchIndicator.test.tsx                                                           |
| Deep Research is a manual toggle in the composer '+' menu, disabled on free trial and when the selected model lacks research support (context window / capability gate).                                                                                                                                       | apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:3688-3702; apps/web/features/chat/lib/research-capability-gate.ts                                            | implemented |                                                                                                                  |
| Deep Research run is bounded: max 6 model-call iterations, 12 total web searches, 4-minute gathering wall clock; url_fetch capped at 8 total / 3 per round / 2 fetch-resolution passes per round.                                                                                                              | apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/research-loop.ts:84-105                                                                                                        | implemented |                                                                                                                  |
| A dedicated planning turn (3-6 planned steps) only runs when the iteration budget can afford plan + gathering + synthesis (>=3 iterations); otherwise the loop keeps its pre-plan shape.                                                                                                                       | apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/research-loop.ts:112-131                                                                                                       | implemented |                                                                                                                  |
| Whatever stops the gathering phase (budget, error, cancel), a synthesis turn always still runs so the user gets a cited report from whatever was collected.                                                                                                                                                    | apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/research-loop.ts:36-38                                                                                                         | implemented |                                                                                                                  |
| Research plan awaiting approval shows Start/Cancel controls; Start is disabled while a decision is in flight.                                                                                                                                                                                                  | apps/web/features/chat/components/research/ResearchActivity.tsx:196-224                                                                                                     | implemented |                                                                                                                  |

| Search/round counts render against their caps only while active ('N of M searches', 'round X of Y'); the cap disappears once the run finishes, leaving a bare count.                                                                                                                                           | apps/web/features/chat/components/research/ResearchActivity.tsx:132-150                                                                                                     | implemented | ResearchActivity.test.tsx covers both states                                                                     |

### States covered

default; hover (CitationChip/SourceRow/SourcesControl bg-hover transitions); focus (Radix Tooltip trigger keyboard focus; focus-visible rings on SourceLinks/PlanStep buttons); active/expanded (ResearchPanel tab aria-selected, ToolTimeline/AgentActivityTimeline expand toggles, ResearchToggleButton open state); disabled (Deep Research MenuToggleRow disabled on free-trial / unsupported model; Start/Retry buttons disabled while in flight); loading ('Searching the web' / 'Loading saved report...' / GlobalSearchDialog Spinner / 'Loading search history...'); streaming (open '[4' marker left unlinked until closed; ResearchActivity ticking clock only while isStreaming); success/complete (research 'complete' phase, 'Searched the web · N sources' summary); failure/error (research 'error' phase, 'Web search didn't return results...' notice, web_search_tool_result_error / gemini grounding failure handling); empty ('No sources yet', 'No saved report yet', 'No results found', GlobalSearchDialog no-query suggestions view); overflow (CitationChip '+N', SourceLinks '+N more', WebSearchCard 'Show more (N more)', SourcesControl 'Sources · N' truncation, 60-char title truncation, 280px tooltip max-width); mobile (ResearchPanel fixed full-screen overlay + backdrop below sm breakpoint vs 360px inline panel on desktop)

### Vendor branches

- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:1176-1200 (appendWebSearchTool)`: Which native web-search tool shape to inject per provider string: anthropic -> web_search_20260209 with allowed_callers:['direct'] and max_uses; google -> {google_search:{}}; openai -> {type:'web_search'}; every other provider gets nothing (falls back to the generic Perplexity tool upstream).
- `packages/ai/providers/google/src/stream.ts:9-60, 195-282`: Gemini-only parsing of groundingMetadata.groundingChunks/groundingSupports and urlContextMetadata into citation-delta + a single server-tool-result('gemini_grounding_result') -- a shape no other provider emits, since Gemini's grounding is not a function call the model explicitly invokes.
- `packages/ai/provider-protocol/src/openai-wire-compat.ts:285-301`: capSearchResultPayload branches on payload.type ('web_search_tool_result' vs 'gemini_grounding_result') to apply the same 5-result render cap through two differently-shaped fields (content array vs results array).
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:1146-1211`: nativeToolCategory/upsertNativeWebSearchEntry special-case name==='gemini_grounding' because Gemini's search has no wire 'started' event, unlike Anthropic/OpenAI's server_tool_use pairing -- so only Gemini gets a client-synthesized activity entry.
- `packages/ai/search/src/web-search-support.ts:32-43 (isWebSearchAvailable)`: Providers with native search (per the model-registry harness catalog: openai, anthropic, google) require modelSupportsNativeSearch===true; every other provider requires modelSupportsTools===true AND the generic backend (Perplexity key) to be configured.
- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:156-220`: An opt-in 'cool' (ChatGPT-style, blue #0b84ff accent) token palette selectable via data-chat-theme='cool' on <html>, layered over the default warm/Claude-leaning palette; currently unreached (no code sets the attribute).

### Generic dashboard smells

- packages/ui/unified-chat's own top-level MessageBubble.tsx/ChatInterface.tsx/MessageList.tsx form a second, fully wired citation+search rendering path (CitationPill, legacy WebSearchCard, ProvenanceFooter) that is exported, tested, and never mounted by the production chat page -- apps/web assembles its own MessageBubble.tsx/ChatComposerNew.tsx instead.
- WebSearchCard.tsx is reachable in the running app only through /dev/inline-toolcall-demo, a developer-only demo route, not any real chat surface.
- packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/SearchOverlay.tsx is dead in production: the shared Sidebar only renders it when no onOpenSearch prop is passed, and the real app always passes one (routing to GlobalSearchDialog instead).
- Two unrelated components share the name 'Sources panel': apps/web/features/projects/components/SourcesPanel.tsx is a Projects knowledge-file upload panel, entirely unrelated to web-search citations, despite the naming overlap with the research Sources panel documented here.
- Two independent [n]-marker citation-linking implementations exist side by side with near-identical fence/inline-code-safe regex logic: citationMarkers.ts (chat messages, '#chat-citation-N') and citation-links.ts (research reports, '#research-source-N') -- duplicated rather than shared.
- data-chat-theme='cool' defines a complete second color palette in chat.css with no code path that ever sets the attribute, so it currently ships as unreachable CSS.

### Open questions

- Did not trace the exact request-processor.ts/tool-loop dispatch point that reads researchEnabled from the client and routes into research-loop.ts vs. the normal single-turn tool loop -- research-loop.ts's existence and its bounds are confirmed, but the trigger condition itself was not read line-by-line.
- ResearchReportsGallery.tsx (Library tab) internals were not inspected -- unconfirmed whether it lists reports via a paginated endpoint distinct from /api/research/reports?limit=1, or reuses the same route with a higher limit.
- No setter for data-chat-theme='cool' was found anywhere in apps/web or packages/ui source; could not rule out it being set by an untracked build-time config, a desktop-app wrapper, or a future surface not yet in this search scope.
- GlobalSearchDialog's click-through behavior (navigating to a result, any 'highlightMessage' scroll/flash treatment in the transcript referenced in a WebChatPage.tsx comment) was not traced end-to-end.
- WEB_SEARCH_MAX_CALLS_PER_TURN=3 (generic Perplexity tool) is enforced server-side, but whether the UI surfaces any explicit feedback when a turn hits that budget (vs. simply stopping further calls silently) was not confirmed.

## Tokens

Design tokens are documented per functional area rather than duplicated here: every
other top-level section in this document carries its own populated
`### Tokens and values` subsection, citing `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css` line
numbers for the `--chat-*` custom properties that area actually renders with. This
section indexes the canonical files and where each area's table lives, it does not
restate the values.

### Entry points

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:1-329`: The canonical `--chat-*` token file. `@layer base { :root { ... } }` (lines 2-103) declares the light-mode defaults, including `--chat-bg: #faf9f7` at line 29; `.dark { ... }` (lines 104-153) overrides the dark-mode values by re-declaring the same property names (several resolved through the `--neutral-*` ramp `foundation.css` defines, e.g. `--chat-bg: hsl(var(--neutral-0))` at line 105); `[data-chat-theme='cool']` (lines 156-221) is a third, opt-in, light-only palette swap layered on top for a ChatGPT-style theme, with no dark counterpart (see Vendor branches, below, and the Open question already recorded under Renderer).
- `apps/web/app/globals.css` and `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/foundation.css`: The surface-level stylesheets every page loading chat.css must also load; foundation.css supplies the `--neutral-*`/`--neutral-fg-*` ramp several `--chat-*` dark-mode values resolve through (e.g. `--chat-text-muted` in dark mode resolves via `--neutral-fg-3`, cited under Models' Tokens and values table).
- Per-area token tables (values, contrast ratios, and citations already recorded, not duplicated here): Shell (lines 85-117), Sidebar (231-260), Composer (368-384), Transcript (518-542), Renderer (656-678), Artifacts (884-906), Models (1014-1037), Files (1177-1200), Connectors (1311-1329), Search (1488-1514).
- `apps/web/shared/components/__tests__/theme-contrast.test.ts`: Computes WCAG contrast ratios directly from the chat.css stylesheet for the token pairings each area's table cites (see Tests, below); this is the enforcement point for the fill/text/on-fill role distinction `.claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md` documents.

### Components

Not applicable, design tokens are CSS custom properties, not components; see each
area's own Components table for which components consume which tokens (e.g. Models'
ComposerFooter row, Renderer's HighlightedCode row).

### Behaviours

Not applicable in the sense the rest of this document uses the word (no independent
runtime behaviour beyond the CSS cascade itself). The one cross-cutting mechanism is
theme selection: `.dark` is toggled at the document root by the app's theme provider,
and `[data-chat-theme='cool']` is toggled independently by the appearance-preferences
surface, so a page can be in any of light/default, dark/default, or light/cool at once
(dark/cool does not exist, see Vendor branches).

### States covered

light (default `:root` values); dark (`.dark` overrides); cool (`[data-chat-theme='cool']`, light-only). Per-token interactive states (hover/focus/disabled surfaces built from these tokens) are covered in each consuming area's own States covered entry, not re-listed here.

### Vendor branches

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:172-217`, selector `html:not(.dark) [data-chat-theme='cool'], html:not(.dark)[data-chat-theme='cool']`: Swaps the code-block, text and accent token values from the warm/Claude-leaning default palette to a ChatGPT-style cool palette, light mode only. There is no `.dark [data-chat-theme='cool']` block anywhere in the file, already flagged as an open question under Renderer (line 709) rather than repeated here.

### Generic dashboard smells

- The document's own structure is the smell this section exists to fix: a reader following the table of contents to "design tokens" found eight empty subsections here (before this pass) while the real, accurate token documentation lived scattered across ten other sections. Anyone extending this document with a new token should add it to the consuming area's `### Tokens and values` table (per the instruction already given at the top of that pattern, e.g. Renderer's note pointing at `theme-contrast.test.ts`), not to this section.

### Tests

- apps/web/shared/components/**tests**/theme-contrast.test.ts, computes contrast ratios from the chat.css stylesheet directly; the single cross-area enforcement point for every `--chat-*-text`/on-fill token pairing cited in the per-area tables.

### Open questions

- Whether a consolidated token reference (one table, every `--chat-*` name, its light/dark/cool values, and which areas consume it) is worth building as a generated artifact from chat.css rather than hand-maintained here; the per-area tables are accurate but partial by construction (each only lists the tokens that area's own audit touched).

## Persistence

### Entry points

- `apps/web/db/neon/0001_mvp_chat.sql:1-60`: Origin schema: creates public.web_conversations and public.web_messages, base columns, indexes, and the update_conversation_on_message trigger.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0037_rls_user_isolation.sql:1-40`: Adds RLS policies scoping both tables to current_app_user_id(); documents that the live query path (getNeonDb() without withUser()) does not set the GUC, so RLS is 'correct-but-dormant' there and app-layer WHERE user_id=$1 remains the active control.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0038_cloud_sync_versioning.sql:1-79`: Adds server_version (shared monotonic cloud_sync_version_seq), updated_at, deleted_at (tombstone) to web_messages and server_version to web_conversations, plus the version-assigning trigger.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0059_conversation_star_archive.sql:1-18`: Adds web_conversations.starred and .archived booleans (default false).
- `apps/web/db/neon/0050_temporary_chat_retention.sql:1-11`: Adds web_conversations.is_temporary for Temporary Chat mode.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0073_tenancy_foundation.sql:134-182`: Adds web_conversations.organization_id (nullable org ownership).
- `apps/web/db/neon/0078_conversation_branching_runtime.sql:1-140`: Makes conversation_branches idempotent (request_id) and adds conversation_branch_messages mapping source->target message copies for forked conversations, with RLS.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0101_sync_and_search_indexes.sql:1-90`: Adds owner-scoped (user_id, server_version) delta indexes on conversations/artifacts/projects/memories; explicitly keeps web_messages on its single-column server_version index because it has no user_id; adds pg_trgm GIN indexes for ILIKE search on web_messages.content and web_conversations.title.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0110_active_workspace_content_scope.sql:1-60`: Defaults web_conversations.organization_id to current_app_org_id(); makes Personal vs org workspace a real content boundary via app_row_is_visible/app_row_is_writable.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0138_retention_enforcement_and_legal_hold.sql:1-110`: Adds organization_admin_policies.retention_enforced (opt-in), legal_holds table, and sweep evidence table; retention sweep permanently deletes conversations past retention_days unless held.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0156_message_thread_variants.sql:1-46`: Adds web_messages.parent_id (thread/variant tree) and web_conversations.active_leaf_message_id (visible-path pointer). Header says NOT YET APPLIED but per user memory this and 0153-0156 are confirmed live in production as of 2026-09-01; header is stale.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0164_memory_policy_and_context_compaction.sql:1-40`: Adds organization_admin_policies.allow_memory and web_conversations.compaction_summary / compaction_summary_through_message_id (cached trim-to-fit-context-window summary). Header also says NOT YET APPLIED/draft.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0021_shared_conversations.sql:1-10`: Legacy shared_conversations table (token/messages_json). Superseded by shared_sessions (0051); confirmed dead outside account-erasure coverage.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0051_shared_sessions.sql:1-17`: Live sharing table: token, owner_id, title, model_id, provider, messages jsonb, total_messages, expires_at.
- `apps/web/db/neon/0068_shared_conversations_owner.sql:1-22`: Adds user_id to the legacy shared_conversations table (BUG-19 fix); GET stays public by design (token is the capability).
- `apps/web/shared/stores/web-chat-store.ts:1-1995 (persist config 1823-1878; Message/MessageMetadata types 181-460; ChatState 500-835)`: Canonical zustand chat store: Message/Conversation/MessageMetadata types, per-conversation message buckets, persist config (partialize/merge/migrate), all message/conversation actions.
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useConversations.ts:1-720`: Primary conversation loader: paginated list fetch, single-conversation paginated load, create/update/delete/restore, toWebConversation wire->store mapper.
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:1-3623 (saveMessageToDb 364-428)`: Send/stream orchestration; saveMessageToDb persists both user and assistant turns client-side via POST .../messages during and after streaming.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/route.ts:1-210`: List (paginated, archived/deleted filters) and create conversation.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/route.ts:1-305`: GET (paginated messages + conversation row), PUT (title/model/project/pinned/starred/archived/isTemporary/activeLeafMessageId), DELETE (soft delete + unpublish artifacts + kill E2B session).
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/restore/route.ts:1-58`: Undelete: clears deleted_at.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/route.ts:1-212`: POST a single message (user/assistant/system); handles thread-lock/parent resolution, auto-title generation, artifact indexing.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/bulk/route.ts:1-167`: POST up to 200 messages at once; used by the 'continue from share' import flow.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/[messageId]/route.ts:1-191`: PATCH (reaction, video-start-failure) merges into messages.metadata; DELETE (single message or whole subtree) with lineage splice/surviving-leaf resolution.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/lib/message-thread.ts:1-~250`: Server-side thread/variant primitives: INSERT_MESSAGE_SQL, lockConversationThread, resolveParentId, resolveAnsweredParentId, collectSubtree, spliceMessageChildren, stampLinearParents, setActiveLeaf.
- `packages/contracts/cloud-contracts/src/message-thread.ts:1-334`: Shared client-side mirror of the thread algorithms (resolveVisibleThread, siblingGroup, variantInfoByMessage, stampLinearParents, subtreeIds) used identically by web/desktop/mobile.
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/branches/route.ts:1-72`: GET branch groups / POST fork-conversation-from-message (whole-conversation branch, distinct from in-thread variants).
- `apps/web/app/api/chat/sync/route.ts:1-818`: Delta cloud-sync GET (pull, cursor=server_version) / POST (push, optimistic-concurrency via baseVersion) for conversations, messages, artifacts.
- `apps/web/features/chat/services/artifact-cloud-sync.ts:1-130`: Web's only client consumer of the sync endpoint; used solely for web_artifacts, not for conversations/messages (those load via the paginated REST endpoints above).
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/assistant-turn-persistence.ts:1-223`: Server-side backstop that persists the assistant turn (content/model/provider/tokens/metadata) if/when the client-side save races or fails; idempotent on assistant_message_id.
- `apps/web/app/api/share/route.ts:1-206`: POST create share (secret redaction, path stripping, 1/7/30-day expiry, external-sharing gate) / GET list own shares.
- `apps/web/app/api/share/[token]/route.ts:1-115`: GET public share view (410 when expired) / DELETE revoke (owner only).
- `apps/web/app/share/[token]/page.tsx:1-79`: Public share viewer page (SSR from shared_sessions).
- `apps/web/app/chat/from-share/[token]/page.tsx:1-147`: 'Continue this conversation' flow: creates a new owned conversation and bulk-imports the shared messages via the bulk route.
- `apps/web/app/api/user/export/route.ts:1-679 (conversations 492-517)`: GDPR Article 20 full account export (JSON, inline or attachment download); includes web_conversations/web_messages among 15+ sections.
- `apps/web/features/chat/services/conversation-export.ts:1-317`: Per-conversation client-side export to Markdown/JSON/HTML/text; also carries unused generateShareLink/copyToClipboard/exportChat/downloadFile methods with no call sites.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/dialogs/EnhancedExportDialog.tsx:1-240+`: Export dialog UI; wires exportAsMarkdown/HTML/JSON plus downloadAsPDF/downloadAsDOCX for a single conversation.
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:3763-4090 (edit/regenerate); 490-596 (message save/delete/active-leaf helpers)`: Orchestrates edit (handleEditMessage/runSubmitEdit) and regenerate (handleRegenerateMessage): variant-mode sibling creation vs legacy destructive replace (sendReplacingMessages), plus local->BYOK trust-boundary refusal and replay-metadata guard.
- `apps/web/features/chat/lib/message-variants-gate.ts:1-69`: Feature flag for edit/regenerate-as-sibling: query param > localStorage > NEXT_PUBLIC_MESSAGE_VARIANTS env, defaults ON.
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:148-220`: Sidebar wiring: star/archive/pin toggles call updateConversation({starred\|archived\|pinned}) which PUTs to the DB-persisted columns.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/context-compaction.ts:28-330`: Reads/writes web_conversations.compaction_summary + boundary id to cache a trimmed-context summary instead of recomputing every turn.
- `apps/web/app/api/cron/purge-temporary-chats/route.ts:n/a (not read in full)`: Purges conversations where is_temporary=true after retention window.
- `apps/web/app/api/cron/enforce-workspace-retention/route.ts:n/a (not read in full)`: Runs the opt-in org retention sweep (0138), honoring legal_holds.

### States covered

loading (isFetchingConversations/isOpeningConversation in useConversations; isLoading/loadingConversationIds in the store); streaming (isStreaming/streamingConversationIds/isStreaming per message; VariantPager disables paging while streaming); empty (0 conversations / 0 messages -- list endpoints and cachedMessages.length checks handle this; UI empty-state not inspected in this pass); failure (listError vs a page-level chat error kept deliberately separate in useConversations; conversationLoadErrorRef maps 404/403 to a distinct message from a generic retry message; streamError/errorCode persisted metadata); success/applied vs conflict (chat/sync push returns per-row applied/conflict with the current server row on conflict); overflow/pagination (hasMore + nextOffset on conversation list and single-conversation message pages; hasMore + saturating cursor math on delta sync pull); offline/retry (429 on conversation list triggers exponential backoff retry up to 5 attempts; online/visibilitychange listeners re-trigger a failed list fetch); mobile (isNarrowViewport media query at 768px in WebAppShell.tsx, gates the mobile nav drawer around the same sidebar that lists these conversations); disabled (VariantPager prev/next disabled at the ends of a sibling group and while streaming)

### Vendor branches

None recorded for this area.

### Generic dashboard smells

- Conversation.isStarred / isArchived are documented in the store's own type comments as 'Client-side only (no DB column in v1)' (apps/web/shared/stores/web-chat-store.ts:473-476), but both are in fact persisted DB columns (starred/archived on web_conversations, added in migration 0059) that round-trip through PUT /api/chat/conversations/[id] -- the type comment is stale and should be fixed or it will mislead the next reader into treating them as ephemeral.
- web_messages.cost_cents exists in the schema, but no write path in apps/web sets it -- the MessageMetadata.cost field's own doc comment confirms 'No producer' and cost stays server-side, so the column is effectively vestigial for the web write path.
- ChatExportService carries a generateShareLink()/copyToClipboard()/exportChat() surface that generates its OWN locally-computed 32-hex-char 'share token' and builds a /share/<token> URL client-side, but nothing in the codebase ever calls these methods and no server record would exist at that token -- dead code that could be confused for the real sharing flow (apps/web/app/api/share/route.ts, which generates and durably stores a different, 24-char base64url token).
- Two migrations relevant to this exact area (0059_conversation_star_archive.sql and 0156_message_thread_variants.sql, and also 0164) carry 'NOT YET APPLIED' / 'draft only, pending approval' headers in their file comments, while application code (route handlers, the store) already reads and writes starred/archived/parent_id/active_leaf_message_id/compaction_summary unconditionally -- per user-provided operating memory, 0153-0156 (and by extension the star/archive and compaction columns) are confirmed live in production as of 2026-09-01 and the stale headers should not be trusted or edited; flagging this so nobody 'fixes' the header back into a not-applied state or assumes the columns are missing.
