### Open questions

- This pass did not read apps/web/lib/server/rls-db.ts / getUserScopedDb() to confirm whether it now calls withUser()/SET LOCAL for the live query path -- 0037's own comment says it did not as of that migration, which matters for whether RLS is actually enforced today versus app-layer WHERE user_id=$1 doing all the work.
- Did not verify the content or retention-day cadence of apps/web/app/api/cron/purge-temporary-chats/route.ts and enforce-workspace-retention/route.ts beyond confirming they exist and reference the right columns/tables -- e.g. actual purge window for is_temporary conversations, and whether the org sweep cron is itself scheduled anywhere (crontab/Vercel cron config not inspected).
- Did not trace how desktop/mobile clients use the /api/chat/sync endpoint for conversations and messages (only confirmed the web app itself uses it solely for artifacts) -- if this map needs to speak to cross-surface sync fidelity, that requires reading the desktop/mobile sync clients too.
- Did not open apps/web/lib/server/neon-chat.ts (ChatMessageRow/ChatConversationRow types, normalizeMessageMetadata) or the ManagedCloudMessageMetadataSchema/ManagedCloudMessageWireSchema definitions in @agiworkforce/cloud-contracts in full -- the MessageMetadata TypeScript interface in the store was used as the primary source for 'what fields the client renders', which should agree with the wire schema but was not diffed field-by-field against it.
- Did not inspect UI-level empty/hover/focus/active states for the conversation list rows (sidebar) or the export dialog buttons -- those statesCovered entries reflect only what the persistence/store/API layer proves, not a visual audit of the sidebar components themselves.

## States

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:727-777, 4380-4664, 4850-4880`: Main signed-in chat page. Assembles Sidebar, ChatComposerNew, ArtifactsPanel, GlobalSearchDialog, KeyboardShortcutsDialog, and the confirm-dialog instance for destructive actions (delete conversation/project). Owns isEmptyChat / isConversationTranscriptPending / isNarrowViewport state that selects the loading-skeleton vs empty vs transcript vs mobile-drawer render branch.
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:1-67, 440-500`: Shared shell for non-/chat routes; reuses the same packages/ui Sidebar and has its own, separately-noted KeyboardShortcutsDialog wiring (top-of-file comment explicitly distinguishes it from WebChatPage's 'richer' wiring).
- `apps/web/app/providers.tsx:41`: Mounts the single app-wide sonner <Toaster position="top-center" richColors closeButton/> that every toast.\* call renders into; also mounts CapabilityProvider from unified-chat.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/ChatLoadingSkeleton.tsx`: The single cold-load spinner, deliberately made the one shared fallback for both app/chat/loading.tsx and WebChatRoot's dynamic() call after the two were found byte-identical duplicates that could drift independently.
- `apps/web/scripts/a11y-audit.mjs`: Standalone Playwright + @axe-core/playwright audit script (wcag2a, wcag2aa, wcag21a, wcag21aa) run signed-out with reducedMotion:'reduce' against the routes in apps/web/lib/a11y/audited-routes.json; writes reports/a11y-report.json and fails the run on any violation or unexpected redirect.
- `apps/web/app/accessibility/ScanScope.tsx`: Public-facing disclosure page that states the audit's scope and limits in prose, generated from the same audited-routes.json so the published claim cannot drift from what is actually scanned.

### States covered

default; hover; focus; focus-visible (distinct from mouse focus); active/expanded (aria-expanded on submenu triggers); disabled (confirm-dialog buttons while busy, in useConfirmAction only); loading (cold skeleton, transcript skeleton, sidebar loading rows); streaming (message.isStreaming branches in MessageBubble); success (SuccessState, toast.success); failure/error (sidebar load-failed, toast.error, ErrorBoundary, image/video generation failure); empty (chat empty state, sidebar no-conversations / no-archived-conversations); overflow (ArtifactsPanel resizable + internal scroll, mermaid-diagram overflow-x:auto, AnchoredComposerMenu clamped/scrolled height); mobile (isNarrowViewport <=768px chat layout switch, ArtifactsPanel isModalOverlay <=639px, Sheet mobile nav drawer); reduced-motion (OS prefers-reduced-motion and in-app data-motion=reduced); high-contrast / forced-colors

### Vendor branches

- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:3620 (role=menu Connectors submenu) vs apps/web/app/chat/projects/[id]/page.tsx:103 / ImageGenerationCard.tsx:708`: Whether a role="menu" panel's keyboard contract comes from the shared useMenuKeyboard hook (projects page, ImageGenerationCard) or from AnchoredComposerMenu's own independent capture-phase Arrow/Home/End/Escape implementation (composer submenus) -- deliberate per the code comment (a second capture-phase listener would fight the first for which item wins), but it means the contract lives in two places with no shared test.
- `WebChatPage.tsx:755 (useConfirm, ConfirmDialog.tsx) vs ArtifactPreview.tsx / GlobalSearchDialog.tsx (useConfirmAction, ConfirmAction.tsx)`: Which of two parallel confirm-dialog primitives handles a destructive action, and therefore whether the confirm button shows a disabled/'Working…' busy state while the async action runs (useConfirmAction) or closes immediately and lets the caller's async work run unobserved (useConfirm, what WebChatPage's delete-conversation/delete-project flows actually use).
- `apps/web chat message components (MessageBubble.tsx, ChatMessageList.tsx, ToolTimeline.tsx) vs packages/ui/unified-chat components (AgentActivityTimeline, StatusTrail, ChatStream, VideoGenCard, ChartArtifact)`: Which reduced-motion hook a given animated chat element reads -- framer-motion's built-in useReducedMotion() in the former, a hand-written matchMedia hook (packages/ui/unified-chat/src/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts) in the latter -- both live in the same rendered message tree (AgentActivityTimeline mounts inside MessageBubble).
- `sonner-backed toast.* calls (live, mounted via apps/web/app/providers.tsx) vs apps/web/shared/stores/notification-store.ts's own showToast/showSuccess/etc.`: Whether a transient message actually reaches the screen. The notification-store branch is fully built (persisted settings, desktop notifications, a synthesized beep) but has zero call sites rendering it, so any code path routed through it silently produces no visible toast.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/dialogs/KeyboardShortcutsDialog.tsx + use-keyboard-shortcuts.ts (imported by WebChatPage/WebAppShell) vs packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/KeyboardShortcutsDialog.tsx / KeyboardShortcutsOverlay.tsx (exported from the package index, zero import sites in apps/web) vs apps/desktop/src/features/chat/KeyboardShortcutsOverlay.tsx (desktop's own separate component)`: Which shortcuts list and which keybindings a user actually sees and can toggle; the three implementations list different, non-matching bindings for overlapping actions (e.g. sidebar toggle is Ctrl/Cmd+B in the live web doc vs Cmd+Shift+S in the unused unified-chat one).

### Generic dashboard smells

- A fully-built second notification/toast subsystem (apps/web/shared/stores/notification-store.ts) with persisted settings, desktop-notification and sound support, wired to nothing -- the kind of dead parallel infrastructure that invites a future author to call the wrong one and get silence.
- Two behaviourally different confirm-dialog primitives (useConfirmAction vs useConfirm) coexisting in the same feature area with no lint/rule enforcing which one a given call site should use, despite the project rule doc naming only useConfirmAction.
- Two unused, hand-authored shortcuts-overlay components in packages/ui/unified-chat that list different keybindings from the live web ones -- inert today, but a landmine if anyone wires them up expecting them to reflect what actually fires.
- The raw-error guard's static test (`grep 'instanceof Error ?'`) is pattern-matched against source text rather than semantic, so `toUserMessage(error, String(error))` slips past it while defeating the guard's own purpose in exactly the same way the guard was written to prevent.
- The published a11y conformance claim (ScanScope.tsx) is honest about its own narrowness, but the gap is still real: zero automated coverage of any authenticated route, meaning the entire chat surface this task maps has never been through the axe pass at all.

### Tests

- apps/web/shared/**tests**/no-raw-error-in-ui.test.ts -- greps for 'instanceof Error ?' in .tsx and asserts toUserMessage() has >20 users; does not catch the String(error)-as-fallback leak found in WebChatPage.tsx
- apps/web/shared/components/**tests**/theme-contrast.test.ts -- computes WCAG contrast ratios from chat.css/globals.css/foundation.css tokens directly
- apps/web/scripts/**tests**/a11y-audit.test.ts -- asserts audited-routes.json never lists a route the signed-out proxy would redirect off of, and exercises auditPage()/findUnexpectedRedirect() against a local fixture server
- apps/web/features/chat/hooks/use-keyboard-shortcuts.test.ts
- apps/web/features/chat/pages/**tests**/WebChatPage.shortcuts.test.tsx
- apps/web/features/chat/hooks/**tests**/shortcut-toggle-wired.test.tsx
- apps/web/features/chat/**tests**/account-menu-shortcut-hint.test.ts
- apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ChatLoadingState.test.tsx
- apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/ArtifactsPanel.shortcut.test.tsx
- apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.test.tsx
- apps/web/lib/**tests**/user-error-message.test.ts
- apps/web/features/chat/components/dialogs/**tests**/GlobalSearchDialog.a11y.test.tsx

### Open questions

- Is the notification-store.ts toast/notification system (apps/web/shared/stores/notification-store.ts) intentionally dormant scaffolding for a future notification-center feature, or genuinely dead code that should be removed -- no ToastContainer/NotificationCenter component was found rendering its state anywhere in apps/web.
- Should WebChatPage's delete-conversation/delete-project flows be migrated from useConfirm to useConfirmAction to match the project rule doc and gain the busy/disabled-while-deleting affordance, or is useConfirm's fire-and-forget-then-close behavior intentional there.
- Are the packages/ui/unified-chat KeyboardShortcutsDialog.tsx / KeyboardShortcutsOverlay.tsx components genuinely unreachable dead exports from apps/web's perspective, or consumed by another surface (Chrome extension, VS Code) not audited here.
- Whether toUserMessage(error, String(error)) at WebChatPage.tsx:1833 and :2731 is a known, accepted gap in the raw-error guard for image/video generation failures specifically, or an oversight worth a follow-up fix plus a test-guard update.
- Whether there is a plan to bring any authenticated route (starting with /chat) under the a11y-audit.mjs script's coverage, given the script's own comment states it currently has no way to run signed in.

## Mobile

### Entry points

- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:760-786 (narrow-viewport state); 4612-4990 (render: shell wrapper, drawer, header, message list, composer, side panels)`: The /chat page: owns isNarrowViewport, the mobile nav Sheet/drawer, the phone header (Menu trigger + collapsed 'Panels' dropdown), and mounts ChatComposerNew + ChatMessageList + ArtifactsPanel + ResearchPanel + WorkSessionPanel.
- `apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:109-134 (narrow-viewport state); 433-500 (render)`: Shell for every authenticated non-/chat route (Projects, Tasks, Settings, etc.) that still uses the shared sidebar. Duplicates WebChatPage's isNarrowViewport/mobileNavOpen pattern with its own testid ('mobile-nav-drawer' vs 'chat-mobile-nav-drawer').
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:420-450 (height/media-query constants); 185-213 (footer key gating); 2660-2664 (safe-area container); 3777-3788 (menu send-route fallback); 4472-4583 (footer disclosure row)`: The composer: textarea auto-resize/compact-height logic, autofocus suppression on touch, safe-area padding, the collapsed footer-disclosure row, and the '+' menu's mobile-only send-route fallback.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:900-937`: Model/reasoning/style selector row rendered inline in the composer control row; hides the response-style selector below sm (640px).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/ArtifactsPanel.tsx:140-142, 194-196, 283, 359-420`: Artifact side panel: full-screen modal overlay with backdrop below 640px, resizable side panel (md/lg width steps) above it.
- `apps/web/features/chat/hooks/use-overlay-dialog.ts:1-28 (useOverlayLayout); 30-96 (useOverlayDialog)`: useOverlayLayout hook: single source of the 639px mobile/desktop split for any panel that covers the page on narrow viewports (Artifacts panel, Research panel). useOverlayDialog adds focus-trap + Escape + focus-restore for the covering (mobile) form.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/ChatMessageList.tsx:333-345 (DateDivider), 1626-1748 (status/typing rows and follow-up shell)`: Message list virtualization/date-dividers/typing-indicator rows; owns the px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20 gutter pattern used by full-width status rows.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/MessageBubble.tsx:1481-1491`: Renders each message; .message-row/.message-inner give the message column its fixed max-w-3xl px-4 gutter shared with the composer.
- `apps/web/app/globals.css:1002-1024 (.message-row/.message-inner); 1492-1512 (.safe-area-bottom-additive); 2436-2489 (consent banner @media 767px/479px + safe-area)`: .message-row/.message-inner definitions; .safe-area-bottom-additive utility (iOS home-indicator padding); consent-banner responsive/safe-area rules.
- `apps/web/app/layout.tsx:75-80`: next Viewport export: viewportFit:'cover' and interactiveWidget:'resizes-content', the app's whole-page answer to the iOS keyboard/safe-area problem; there is no JS visualViewport listener anywhere in the repo.
- `apps/web/shared/components/CookieConsent.tsx:81-109`: Measures its own rendered height and writes it into --agi-consent-inset on <html>, which both shells subtract from their fixed-height wrapper so the banner never covers the composer or a route's primary CTA.
- `apps/web/e2e/chat-sidebar-drawer.spec.ts:1-189, entire file`: Browser-level spec for the /chat mobile nav drawer at 390x844: open/focus, Escape/focus-return, overlay click-to-close, the row action menu's operability inside the Radix Sheet, and select-to-navigate.
- `apps/web/e2e/qa-08-target-size.spec.ts:1-80, entire file`: WCAG 2.5.8 24x24 target-size sweep at 390px and 1440px across /chat, /chat/projects, /chat/library, /tasks, /agi-work.
- `apps/web/e2e/qa-16-consent-overlay.spec.ts:1-108, entire file`: Verifies the consent banner never blocks the Stop-generation control or a route's primary CTA at 390x844 (and 1440x900).
- `apps/web/e2e/qa-05-responsive.spec.ts:9-13 (VIEWPORTS), 99-147 (test body)`: Horizontal-overflow-only sweep across 375/768/1440 for ~15 routes; the only spec that names a 768px 'tablet' viewport, and it only checks doc.scrollWidth, not layout/interaction.
- `apps/web/e2e/authenticated-flows.spec.ts:344-375`: One assertion at 390x844 (overflow<=1px on /chat) inside a broader signed-in a11y/responsive test; the rest of the test runs at 1280x800.

### Behaviours

### States covered

default (phone drawer closed, sidebar hidden) - chat-sidebar-drawer.spec.ts; open/expanded (drawer open, focus inside) - chat-sidebar-drawer.spec.ts; focus (drawer trigger focus-visible ring; textarea focus/autofocus suppression) - ChatComposerNew.tsx global focus-visible utility classes; takeIdleFocus gating; active/streaming (Stop-generation button reachable under the consent banner while a response streams) - qa-16-consent-overlay.spec.ts; loading (account/footer skeleton in WebAppShell footerSlot, animate-pulse) - WebAppShell.tsx:322-334; disabled (hard-disabled coming-soon/env-locked model rows in ComposerFooter) - ComposerFooter.tsx:463-471 (desktop-agnostic, not mobile-specific); empty (sidebar 'No conversations yet' text) - Sidebar.tsx (packages/ui/unified-chat variant); not directly proven for the drawer-mounted shared Sidebar at phone width; overflow (WCAG 24x24 target-size sweep at 390px catches undersized controls) - qa-08-target-size.spec.ts; overflow (horizontal page-scroll sweep at 375/768/1440, non-blocking) - qa-05-responsive.spec.ts; mobile-collapsed (composer footer disclosure reduced to one entry) - ChatComposerNew.mobileFooter.test.tsx; mobile-compact (composer column padding/row-gap/control-row height reduced) - ChatComposerNew.density.test.tsx; modal-overlay (artifact panel as full-screen dialog with backdrop) - ArtifactsPanel.tsx (implemented, not test-covered per findings above)

### Vendor branches

- `ChatComposerNew.tsx:194-206 (DESK_ONLY_COMPOSER_FOOTER_KEYS doc-comment)`: What entries the mobile composer footer drops, decided against a documented live measurement: chatgpt.com shows one disclosure line above its composer at 390px, claude.ai shows nothing there at all; this codebase's own prior state ran to three lines / 136px. The fix keeps only the accuracy disclaimer, citing both competitors' patterns.
- `ComposerFooter.tsx:929-932 (comment on hiding StyleSelector below sm)`: Style selector is dropped below sm 'like claude.ai's mobile composer' so the model picker keeps a usable width, explicit parity citation, not a generic responsive default.
- `WebChatPage.tsx:4405-4407 (AGI Work goal-intake fields, canUseAgiWork branch)`: References claude.ai's Chat/Cowork axis as the interaction model for the Chat\|AGI Work segmented toggle sitting next to the composer '+' button; not phone/tablet-specific but the toggle inherits the same composer density rules.

### Generic dashboard smells

- Two independent, hand-duplicated implementations of the same narrow-viewport sidebar/drawer pattern (WebChatPage.tsx and WebAppShell.tsx each define their own isNarrowViewport state, matchMedia listener, mobileNavOpen state, and near-identical Sheet markup with different testids) rather than one shared hook/component, a change to the 768px threshold or the drawer width has to be made twice and could silently drift, as the width already has (280 via a named constant in one file, 280 as a bare literal in the other).
- Two different mobile-layout breakpoints govern visually adjacent chrome with no visible seam: the sidebar/header/composer-footer collapse at 768px (md) while the composer's own row-density/height collapse and the artifact/research panel's overlay-vs-side-panel switch happen at 640px (sm). A 641-768px viewport (most of the 'tablet' range) therefore renders the phone-density composer and the phone drawer/header simultaneously with the desktop-width artifact side panel, a state no spec or component test exercises.
- The message column's own gutter (.message-inner, fixed px-4 via max-w-3xl) and the auxiliary full-width status rows' gutter (ChatMessageList.tsx's px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20 pattern) are two different responsive strategies aiming at the same visual edge, maintained as separate hard-coded class strings in at least 6 call sites (lines 335, 1626, 1640, 1656, 1685, 1718) plus one that diverges from the pattern (line 1748's follow-up shell is flat px-4, no md/lg step), a change to the desktop indent has 7 places to update and one of them already reads differently from the rest.

### Tests

- apps/web/e2e/chat-sidebar-drawer.spec.ts, browser-level, 390x844, full drawer lifecycle (open/focus, Escape/focus-return, overlay-click, row-menu operability inside the Radix Sheet body-lock, select-to-navigate)
- apps/web/e2e/qa-08-target-size.spec.ts, browser-level, 390px and 1440px, WCAG 24x24 target size across /chat, /chat/projects, /chat/library, /tasks, /agi-work
- apps/web/e2e/qa-16-consent-overlay.spec.ts, browser-level, 390x844 and 1440x900, consent banner never blocks Stop-generation or a route's primary CTA (also covers WebAppShell routes /chat/schedules, /chat/library)
- apps/web/e2e/qa-05-responsive.spec.ts, browser-level, 375/768/1440, horizontal-overflow-only sweep across ~15 authenticated routes (non-blocking: records offenders via console.log, does not fail the test on overflow)
- apps/web/e2e/authenticated-flows.spec.ts, browser-level, single 390x844 overflow<=1px assertion on /chat inside a larger a11y test that otherwise runs at 1280x800
- apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.mobileFooter.test.tsx, jsdom component test, matchMedia mocked to md=768px, asserts the collapsed footer's visible/hidden entry set and the '+' menu send-route fallback via CSS class presence only (no real media-query evaluation, by its own doc-comment)
- apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.density.test.tsx, jsdom component test, asserts the composer's sm:-paired density classes and (via a stubbed matchMedia) the JS-pinned mobile textarea height
- apps/web/e2e/qa-05-responsive.spec.ts (VIEWPORTS list), the only spec anywhere under apps/web/e2e that names a 768x1024 'tablet' viewport explicitly; no other spec distinguishes tablet from phone or from desktop

### Open questions

- No e2e spec re-measures ChatComposerNew's actual rendered pixel height, footer-entry count, or control-row button size at a real 390px (or any phone/tablet) browser viewport, the two dedicated test files that cover this behaviour (density.test.tsx, mobileFooter.test.tsx) are jsdom-only and each say outright, in their own comments, that jsdom cannot evaluate the media queries under test and 'the pixel outcome belongs to a browser run.' Per .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md's own stated standard ('a jsdom pass is not a browser pass'), this looks like exactly the gap that rule was written to close, but I could not find the closing spec.
- No spec exercises the 641-768px band where the composer/panel breakpoint (640px) and the sidebar/header breakpoint (768px) disagree, is a ~700px viewport (a small Android tablet in portrait, or a folded foldable) an intentionally accepted mixed state, or an unconsidered gap?
- No test found for ArtifactsPanel's mobile modal-overlay branch (backdrop, role='dialog', focus containment) at either the component or e2e level, I could not confirm from tests alone that useOverlayLayout's mobile branch renders and behaves as documented; this is inferred from reading the component and hook source only.
- composer-editor.spec.ts and composer-editor-ime.spec.ts (mention picker, slash commands, IME composition) run only at the default desktop viewport; whether the mention/slash dropdown menus or IME candidate window interact correctly with the compact 36px input row or the on-screen keyboard is unverified by any spec I found.
- The follow-up-suggestions shell (ChatMessageList.tsx:1748) uses a flat px-4 gutter with no md:/lg: step, unlike the date-divider and typing-indicator rows two blocks above it in the same file (px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20), I could not determine from the code alone whether this is intentional (the shell may already sit inside a max-w-3xl-constrained ancestor) or a drift from the surrounding pattern; would need a rendered-DOM check to resolve.

## Vendor branches across the chat UI

Deduplicated across every area above: the same `where` location is listed once, even if more than one area's map recorded it.

- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:156-165, 172-173 ([data-chat-theme='cool'] selector)`: An opt-in ChatGPT-style ('cool') light/dark palette override layered on top of the default warm/Claude-leaning palette, not vendor-detection per se, but a deliberately named ChatGPT-parity theme branch a reader should know exists before assuming --chat-bg always resolves to the warm value.
- `apps/web/proxy.ts: buildAgiCodeResponse/AGI_CODE_PATH vs buildAgiWorkResponse/AGI_WORK_PATH`: Two differently-branded entry surfaces ('/agi-work' -> /chat with agiwork composer mode, '/agi-code' -> /chat/code) rewritten for signed-in users only, not a third-party vendor branch, but worth flagging as the closest thing to a mode/vendor fork in this area.
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:4570-4646 vs apps/web/shared/components/layout/WebAppShell.tsx:395-500`: Not a vendor/provider branch but a duplicated-wiring split: the same Sidebar component is independently instantiated once for /chat (richer: usage widget, streaming/running state, custom-instructions) and once for every other route via WebAppShell (thinner). sidebar-session-actions.ts exists specifically to keep the two delete-confirm copies from drifting; app-nav-items.ts keeps the nav rail array from drifting. No other divergence was checked in this pass.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/sidebar/Sidebar.tsx vs packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`: Two structurally different Sidebar implementations exist in the monorepo. packages/ui/ui's is the one actually rendered in production (verified via import chain from apps/web/app/chat routes). packages/ui/unified-chat's Sidebar/useSidebar/ConversationItem/uiStore is a parallel, less-capable implementation (no menu, no pin/star/archive/share/move actions, different collapsed width 48px vs 64px, different persisted store key) with no production import site found under apps/web/app or apps/web/features.
- `Menu.tsx:135-172 computePosition()`: portalled (default, used by every sidebar menu trigger) vs non-portalled positioning branch; the non-portalled path is a single-shift correction for a caller outside the sidebar (documented as the connectors table), not exercised by sidebar call sites
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:1053-1060 (isWebSearchAvailable) and 1065-1071 (providerHasNativeCodeExecution list ['anthropic','google','openai'])`: Whether the ambient web-search status and the Run-code plus-menu toggle are available, branching on the selected model's provider string and its models.json capability flags rather than a single generic 'tools' flag.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:373-411 (ProviderLogo / toProviderId / providerBrandHex)`: Which provider mark renders in the model-picker trigger and rows: a special AGI mark for managed_cloud (Auto modes), else the shared ProviderMark component when hasProviderMark(providerKey) is true, else a local /providers/<id>.svg with a brand-hex fallback dot.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:221-250 (modelLock)`: A single combined lock decision -- availability ('coming_soon') first, then tier, then requiresEnvironment -- consumed by every call site (search, partition, inline 'More models', reset effect) so no one branch can drift from another; comment flags this as a fix for a prior partial-update gating leak.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:255-264 (isHighUsageRateModel)`: Whether a model row shows the 'consumes usage limits faster' tooltip, now sourced from getPickerModelTier(model.id) instead of a prior display-name substring match on the word 'opus' (called out in-code as AUDIT-FIX CMP-25).
- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:150-224 (html:not(.dark) [data-chat-theme='cool'])`: Opt-in ChatGPT-style ('cool') light palette vs. the default warm/Claude-leaning palette that both MessageBubble and ChatMessageList read exclusively through --chat-\* variables -- no vendor branch lives in the TSX itself, it is entirely a token swap.
- `ChatMessageList.tsx:60-71 (isRefusalFinish)`: Normalizes two different provider wire vocabularies for a safety stop -- the canonical 'refusal' and the OpenAI-wire-compatible 'content_filter' -- into the same single refusal notice, so the UI never branches on which provider produced the turn.
- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:172-217, selector `html:not(.dark) [data-chat-theme='cool'], html:not(.dark)[data-chat-theme='cool']``: Swaps the code-block, text and accent token values from the warm/Claude-leaning default palette to a ChatGPT-style cool palette (light mode only, no dark counterpart exists for 'cool').
- `apps/web/app/globals.css:1137-1150, `html[data-chat-font='serif'|'sans'|'dyslexic'] .prose``: Overrides the prose font-family (and forces code back to monospace) per a user-selected reading-preference vendor-agnostic setting, unrelated to the ChatGPT/Claude theme branch.
- `shikiHighlighter.ts:117-124, THEME_LOADERS = { light: github-light, dark: github-dark }`: Which of two fixed Shiki themes tokens resolve against; not tied to data-chat-theme='cool', so Shiki token colours do NOT follow the cool/warm chat palette, only the light/dark mode.
- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:157-213, selector [data-chat-theme='cool'] opt-in`: A ChatGPT-style 'cool' blue-accent palette (--chat-accent-primary:#0b84ff, --chat-text-primary:#0d0d0d) as an alternate to the default warm/amber Claude-style theme (--chat-accent-primary:#da7756), an explicit opt-in data attribute, not automatic vendor detection.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/ArtifactsPanel.tsx vs packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ArtifactPanel.tsx`: Web ships its own bespoke panel/preview/store implementation; desktop uses the shared unified-chat ArtifactPanel + its own ArtifactCategoryFilter/ArtifactVersionHistory/ShareArtifactDialog. Not a runtime branch, two parallel implementations selected at the app level.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/artifacts/publishedArtifactRender.ts isSandboxedPublishedKind()`: Whether a published-artifact kind (html/react/mermaid vs svg/markdown/code/text) is rendered through the cross-origin sandbox iframe or inertly, the actual security boundary for the public share page.
- `packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ModelSelector.tsx:516, 519`: Splits displayModels into autoModels (provider==='managed_cloud' or isAutoModeModelId) vs providerModels for separate rendering sections in the shared-package picker (not used by apps/web's chat page).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ComposerFooter.tsx:100 (toProviderId)`: providerKey==='managed_cloud' maps to the 'agi-cloud' ProviderId for PROVIDER_DISPLAY lookup; otherwise passes the key through only if it exists in PROVIDER_DISPLAY, else null (no icon).
- `ComposerFooter.tsx:355-357 (partitionModels isAuto)`: m.providerKey==='managed_cloud' identifies the Auto row(s) so they sort first in both the Available and More-models groups.
- `ComposerFooter.tsx:369-410 (ProviderLogo)`: providerKey==='managed_cloud' renders the AgiMark brand mark; otherwise tries ProviderMark (hasProviderMark), then a local /providers/<id>.svg <img>, then a plain brand-color dot as last resort.
- `packages/ui/ui/src/ProviderMark.tsx:29 (hasProviderMark) and :44 (component)`: key==='openai' renders a hardcoded inline OpenAI SVG path (not in the simple-icons ICON_BY_PROVIDER map); every other key looks up ICON_BY_PROVIDER (siClaude for anthropic/claude, siGooglegemini for google/gemini, siDeepseek, siMoonshotai for moonshot/kimi, siOllama, siPerplexity, siQwen, siMistralai, siX for xai/grok); an unmapped key renders nothing.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:798`: modelSupportsEffort() fallback (when the model isn't in the catalog): provider==='anthropic'\|\|'openai'\|\|'google' → true, else false, gates whether an effort value is forwarded at all.
- `request-processor.ts:869-872`: provider==='openai' additionally requires supportsOpenAIReasoningEffort() before an effort value survives, OpenAI-only extra gate on top of the generic capability check.
- `request-processor.ts:879`: anthropicUsesAdaptiveThinking(): provider!=='anthropic' short-circuits to false, adaptive-thinking budgeting logic is Anthropic-only.
- `request-processor.ts:914`: provider!=='anthropic' returns undefined from the thinking-budget builder, non-Anthropic requests never get a {type,budget_tokens} thinking block.
- `request-processor.ts:1206`: modelHasNativeAnthropicWebFetch(): provider==='anthropic' && providerCompatibility.nativeWebFetch!==false gates whether the native web_fetch tool is offered vs a generic fetch tool.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/canonical-request.ts:56, 68, 106, 121, 130, 188`: Provider-specific request-shape branches: openrouter/open_router model-id passthrough, anthropic-only fields (x2), google-only field, openai-only field, pure request-body construction, not UI.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/context-compaction.ts:125; app/api/chat/conversations/[id]/messages/lib/generate-title.ts:168; lib/support/agent/answer/model-route.ts:76; lib/services/scheduled-agent-executor.ts:334`: Four independent copies of the same check, route.provider==='anthropic'\|\|'google', gating which routes are eligible for a given backend feature (context compaction, title generation, support-agent answers, scheduled-agent execution), a repeated literal rather than a shared helper.
- `apps/web/lib/cost-tracker.ts:55; apps/web/lib/prompt-cache-helper.ts:36`: meta.provider==='anthropic' selects Anthropic's distinct cache-token accounting formula (input+cacheRead+cacheWrite vs plain input) for cost/usage math, backend billing logic, not rendering.
- `apps/web/lib/server/container-files.ts:155-158`: ref.provider==='openai' vs 'anthropic' selects which provider's file-reference resolution path to use for container-generated files.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/messages/cards/MapSearchCard.tsx:280`: action.provider==='google_maps' selects the primary action button among a search result's actions, the one UI-rendering branch on a vendor value in a message card (falls back to actions[0]).
- `apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx:667`: Validates a persisted video job's provider is one of 'google'\|'runway'\|'openrouter' before restoring it into message metadata on reload, a data-shape guard, not a rendering fork.
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useMediaGeneration.ts:299, 362, 365-367`: Image/video generation polling hook: validates and normalizes provider ('open_router'→'openrouter' display key) to decide which poll/complete branch and provider label to show while generation is in-flight, feeds UI state (progress/labels) though the branch itself is a data hook, not JSX.
- `apps/web/app/api/media/image/generate/route.ts:249-350, 1386 (switch); apps/web/app/api/media/video/generate/route.ts:310-1294; apps/web/lib/services/video-provider-output-service.ts (multiple); apps/web/lib/services/media-model-availability-service.ts:55-94`: A large family of provider==='openai'\|'google'\|'runway'\|'openrouter' branches selecting request shape, polling endpoint, and pricing/duration formula for image/video generation, entirely server-side route/service logic, not UI rendering.
- `packages/ai/provider-runtime/src/history.ts:33,108-113,211,235 (policy: 'anthropic-shape' | 'openai-shape')`: How image/document/file content blocks in conversation history are shaped before being handed to a provider adapter; a media-pruning routine (line ~161-208) also drops the oldest image/document/pdf blocks first when trimming history for context length, before dropping text.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/chat-attachment-hydration.ts (isChatImageMimeType branch at line 386 vs. generic 'file' branch at 419-444)`: Whether a resolved attachment becomes an image_url data-URI part (base64) or a file/file_data part with a mime_type - this shape is provider-family-agnostic at this layer; the anthropic-shape/openai-shape translation happens further downstream in packages/ai.
- `apps/web/features/directory/services/connectors-directory.ts:30-41,73-76 (BADGE_TO_KIND, CURATED_BADGE/SELF_ADDED_BADGE, CONNECTABLE_BLOCKED)`: Whether a connector renders as verified/community/'yours', and whether its Add control shows at all (installable:false for connectable modes 'desktop-and-cli' and 'needs-setup', i.e. connectors that require the desktop app or manual setup can't be connected from web).
- `apps/web/lib/connectors/directory/sources/first-party.json + FIRST_PARTY_TARGETS_BY_ID (connectors-directory.ts:43-56)`: For curated (first-party) connectors, supplies the tool-name list, documentation URL and connector URL shown in the detail pane, derived from a static per-connector JSON manifest rather than the live registry record.
- `apps/web/lib/connectors/scope-descriptions.ts:26-260 (SCOPE_DESCRIPTIONS)`: Per-provider (Google Gmail/Calendar/Drive/Sheets/Analytics/YouTube/BigQuery/Cloud-Storage, Microsoft Graph, Meta/Instagram/Facebook Pages, SMART-on-FHIR) canonical OAuth scope strings map to a plain-language sentence and a read/write access class; an unrecognized scope for a known connector falls back to a generic 'This permission has not been described yet.' (classified write, i.e. fails safe toward the stricter badge).
- `apps/web/features/directory/services/skills-directory.ts:38-46 (OWNED_SOURCES, skillPublisher)`: Whether a skill's publisher line reads 'Yours' (personal/project/workspace source), 'Managed' (managed-local), or 'Made by AGI' (anything else).
- `apps/web/features/directory/services/plugins-directory.ts:61,102-105 (firstParty = publisher.kind === 'first-party')`: Whether a plugin files under the 'Made by AGI' or 'Partners' source chip.
- `apps/web/app/api/llm/v1/chat/completions/lib/request-processor.ts:1176-1200 (appendWebSearchTool)`: Which native web-search tool shape to inject per provider string: anthropic -> web_search_20260209 with allowed_callers:['direct'] and max_uses; google -> {google_search:{}}; openai -> {type:'web_search'}; every other provider gets nothing (falls back to the generic Perplexity tool upstream).
- `packages/ai/providers/google/src/stream.ts:9-60, 195-282`: Gemini-only parsing of groundingMetadata.groundingChunks/groundingSupports and urlContextMetadata into citation-delta + a single server-tool-result('gemini_grounding_result') -- a shape no other provider emits, since Gemini's grounding is not a function call the model explicitly invokes.
- `packages/ai/provider-protocol/src/openai-wire-compat.ts:285-301`: capSearchResultPayload branches on payload.type ('web_search_tool_result' vs 'gemini_grounding_result') to apply the same 5-result render cap through two differently-shaped fields (content array vs results array).
- `apps/web/lib/hooks/useChatStream.ts:1146-1211`: nativeToolCategory/upsertNativeWebSearchEntry special-case name==='gemini_grounding' because Gemini's search has no wire 'started' event, unlike Anthropic/OpenAI's server_tool_use pairing -- so only Gemini gets a client-synthesized activity entry.
- `packages/ai/search/src/web-search-support.ts:32-43 (isWebSearchAvailable)`: Providers with native search (per the model-registry harness catalog: openai, anthropic, google) require modelSupportsNativeSearch===true; every other provider requires modelSupportsTools===true AND the generic backend (Perplexity key) to be configured.
- `packages/ui/design-tokens/src/chat.css:156-220`: An opt-in 'cool' (ChatGPT-style, blue #0b84ff accent) token palette selectable via data-chat-theme='cool' on <html>, layered over the default warm/Claude-leaning palette; currently unreached (no code sets the attribute).
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/ChatComposerNew.tsx:3620 (role=menu Connectors submenu) vs apps/web/app/chat/projects/[id]/page.tsx:103 / ImageGenerationCard.tsx:708`: Whether a role="menu" panel's keyboard contract comes from the shared useMenuKeyboard hook (projects page, ImageGenerationCard) or from AnchoredComposerMenu's own independent capture-phase Arrow/Home/End/Escape implementation (composer submenus) -- deliberate per the code comment (a second capture-phase listener would fight the first for which item wins), but it means the contract lives in two places with no shared test.
- `WebChatPage.tsx:755 (useConfirm, ConfirmDialog.tsx) vs ArtifactPreview.tsx / GlobalSearchDialog.tsx (useConfirmAction, ConfirmAction.tsx)`: Which of two parallel confirm-dialog primitives handles a destructive action, and therefore whether the confirm button shows a disabled/'Working…' busy state while the async action runs (useConfirmAction) or closes immediately and lets the caller's async work run unobserved (useConfirm, what WebChatPage's delete-conversation/delete-project flows actually use).
- `apps/web chat message components (MessageBubble.tsx, ChatMessageList.tsx, ToolTimeline.tsx) vs packages/ui/unified-chat components (AgentActivityTimeline, StatusTrail, ChatStream, VideoGenCard, ChartArtifact)`: Which reduced-motion hook a given animated chat element reads -- framer-motion's built-in useReducedMotion() in the former, a hand-written matchMedia hook (packages/ui/unified-chat/src/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts) in the latter -- both live in the same rendered message tree (AgentActivityTimeline mounts inside MessageBubble).
- `sonner-backed toast.* calls (live, mounted via apps/web/app/providers.tsx) vs apps/web/shared/stores/notification-store.ts's own showToast/showSuccess/etc.`: Whether a transient message actually reaches the screen. The notification-store branch is fully built (persisted settings, desktop notifications, a synthesized beep) but has zero call sites rendering it, so any code path routed through it silently produces no visible toast.
- `apps/web/features/chat/components/dialogs/KeyboardShortcutsDialog.tsx + use-keyboard-shortcuts.ts (imported by WebChatPage/WebAppShell) vs packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/KeyboardShortcutsDialog.tsx / KeyboardShortcutsOverlay.tsx (exported from the package index, zero import sites in apps/web) vs apps/desktop/src/features/chat/KeyboardShortcutsOverlay.tsx (desktop's own separate component)`: Which shortcuts list and which keybindings a user actually sees and can toggle; the three implementations list different, non-matching bindings for overlapping actions (e.g. sidebar toggle is Ctrl/Cmd+B in the live web doc vs Cmd+Shift+S in the unused unified-chat one).
- `ChatComposerNew.tsx:194-206 (DESK_ONLY_COMPOSER_FOOTER_KEYS doc-comment)`: What entries the mobile composer footer drops, decided against a documented live measurement: chatgpt.com shows one disclosure line above its composer at 390px, claude.ai shows nothing there at all; this codebase's own prior state ran to three lines / 136px. The fix keeps only the accuracy disclaimer, citing both competitors' patterns.
- `ComposerFooter.tsx:929-932 (comment on hiding StyleSelector below sm)`: Style selector is dropped below sm 'like claude.ai's mobile composer' so the model picker keeps a usable width, explicit parity citation, not a generic responsive default.
- `WebChatPage.tsx:4405-4407 (AGI Work goal-intake fields, canUseAgiWork branch)`: References claude.ai's Chat/Cowork axis as the interaction model for the Chat\|AGI Work segmented toggle sitting next to the composer '+' button; not phone/tablet-specific but the toggle inherits the same composer density rules.

## Open questions

Grouped by area, in the same order as the sections above.

### Shell

- ChatLoadingSkeleton (apps/web/features/chat/components/ChatLoadingSkeleton.tsx) doesn't use the shared Spinner primitive (packages/ui/ui/src/primitives/Spinner.tsx) called out by .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md; it has its own aria-live/aria-busy/aria-label handling but its inline animation is not covered by the global prefers-reduced-motion .animate-spin rule (apps/web/app/globals.css:2237-2242), worth confirming whether that's an intentional exception (route-level fallback, no store/i18n available yet) or a gap.
- WebChatPage's account-footer loading treatment (aria-busy + disabled button, email hidden) differs visually from WebAppShell's dedicated animate-pulse skeleton block for the same slot, not verified live in a browser, only from source, so it's possible this reads fine but is worth a visual check across both shells side by side.
- Did not verify a genuine 'tablet' intermediate breakpoint distinct from the binary 768px sidebar-drawer split and the 639px panel-overlay split, the responsive behaviour appears to be exactly two step functions (mobile drawer <=768px, panel-overlay <640px) rather than three tiers; not confirmed live in a browser at e.g. 820px (iPad-portrait) width.
- Sidebar expanded width: neither WebChatPage nor WebAppShell pass an explicit `width` prop to the persistent (non-collapsed, non-mobile) <Sidebar>, so it renders at the component's own default of 260px, did not find an override anywhere in this area, but did not exhaustively grep every call site outside apps/web/features/chat and apps/web/shared/components/layout.
- Did not inspect apps/web/app/chat/from-share/[token]/page.tsx or apps/web/features/chat/pages/WebChatPage.tsx's AGI Work / AGI Code composer-toggle rendering in detail, out of scope for the shell/header/width mapping requested, flagging only that it exists as an entry point.

### Sidebar

- Where is onOpenCustomInstructions wired from WebChatPage/WebAppShell to SessionItem (what UI does it open)? Not traced in this pass.
- Does Menu.tsx's inline keyboard handling deliberately diverge from primitives/useMenuKeyboard.ts, or is it an untracked duplicate that predates that hook? Worth a git-blame comparison of the two files' dates before treating this as a defect.
- Is packages/ui/unified-chat's Sidebar/ChatInterface genuinely dead code (no import anywhere, e.g. Storybook, a different app, or a future surface), or reachable from some entry point outside apps/web that this pass did not search?
- Contrast against `apps/web/e2e/qa-10-overlay-blocking.spec.ts` and `qa-07-interaction-crawl.spec.ts` (both grep-matched 'sidebar' but were not opened) for any additional sidebar-adjacent coverage or gaps.

### Composer

- SendButton's 'queue' mode (Clock icon, terra-cotta styling) is fully implemented in SendButton.tsx but ChatComposerNew's sendButtonMode is a strict isTurnActive?'stop':'send' ternary -- confirm whether any other composer surface actually mounts SendButton with mode='queue', or whether this is dead code / a mouse-accessibility gap for queuing during a stream (Enter key still works).
- apps/web/features/chat/components/Composer/StyleSelector.tsx, VoiceInputButton.tsx, VoiceRecordingOverlay.tsx, and ComposerFeedbackDialog.tsx were only read by reference/grep, not fully audited line-by-line -- their internal state machines (recording states, style-picker options) are not covered here.
- The submit-keymap's Mod-Enter branch always submits regardless of resolveSendShortcut(), while plain Enter respects it; no current caller passes ComposerEditor a non-default sendShortcut, so this divergence is currently dormant but worth flagging if a send-shortcut preference is ever wired up.
- ComposerFooter.tsx is 1217 lines and was not read in full (roughly lines 1-660 and 930-1217 were read closely; 660-930 and end-of-file sections beyond 1213 were sampled via grep rather than read verbatim) -- StyleSelector integration details and the very end of the file were not verified line-by-line.

### Transcript

- Regenerate has no per-click model choice anywhere in this surface (icon button always resends under the conversation's current activeModelId); confirm with the task owner whether 'regenerate with model choice' was meant to exist here or is being planned separately, since nothing in ChatMessageList/MessageBubble/WebChatPage wires one.
- Whether the px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20 gutter on the transcript-footer notices/DateDivider (vs. the fixed px-4 max-w-3xl mx-auto message column) is an intentional wider band or an unnoticed drift -- worth a live check on a >=1024px viewport with a Stopped/StreamError notice showing.
- Whether TypingIndicator's missing width wrapper and lack of an aria-live/role is a known gap or worth a quick fix given the project's own rule explicitly bans hand-rolled loading spinners of this shape.
- Whether apps/web/features/chat/chat-interface.css (unimported, dead) should be deleted outright to remove the conflicting duplicate .message-bubble animation rule.

### Renderer

- Whether MarkdownLite's lack of rehype-sanitize is an accepted risk given its current callers (PresentationArtifact, EmailArtifact slide/email bodies are model-authored content) or an oversight, it was not in scope to trace whether those bodies can carry raw HTML from the model.
- Whether the missing dark-mode variant of [data-chat-theme='cool'] is intentional (cool theme is light-only by design) or an unfinished token set, chat.css has no `.dark [data-chat-theme='cool']` block at all.
- Whether line-number support for code blocks is planned; no trace of it (implementation, token, or even a dead flag) exists anywhere under packages/ui/unified-chat/markdown, codeBlock.css, or the appearance-preferences surface.

### Artifacts

- Whether packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/ArtifactPanel.tsx (with ArtifactCategoryFilter, ArtifactVersionHistory, ArtifactToolbar, ShareArtifactDialog siblings under apps/desktop/src/features/artifacts/) has any live web consumer beyond desktop was not fully exhaustively grepped outside apps/web and apps/desktop, worth a repo-wide check (extension-vscode, mobile) if parity matters.
- packages/ui/unified-chat/src/components/artifact-components/ArtifactSandboxFrame.tsx exists alongside apps/web's own SandboxedIframe.tsx, not confirmed which (if either) apps/web actually renders through; ArtifactPreview.tsx imports the web-local SandboxedIframe, so ArtifactSandboxFrame looks unified-chat/desktop-only, but this wasn't traced further.

- hasGeneratedFileManifest / GeneratedFileCard privacy-label rendering (Shield icon flows for tool-generated files attached to an artifact) was read only at the call-site level in ArtifactPreview.tsx, not inside GeneratedFileCard itself.

### Models

- Is packages/ui/unified-chat's ModelSelector/AgentControl/ProvenanceFooter stack intended as the future replacement for apps/web's ComposerFooter, or a permanently separate desktop/mobile implementation? No shared abstraction currently keeps their Auto label, streaming-disable behavior, or served-by footer content in sync.
- apps/web/shared/stores/model-store.ts:192-197 hardcodes getAllowedAutoModesForTier() to always return ['auto'] regardless of tier, is that intentional (the catalog only ever exposes one selectable Auto alias today) or a placeholder for the auto-economy/auto-balanced/auto-premium aliases that exist in routing-policies.json but are marked selectable:false?
- The x_agi_workforce.routing block in response-builder.ts (task_type/task_confidence/resolved_model/slot) is emitted on the OpenAI-compatible /v1 API but not read or displayed anywhere in the chat UI found, confirm whether any consumer (dashboards, API users) actually reads it, or whether it is dead transparency.
- ComposerFooter's model list has no client-visible loading/error state because AVAILABLE_MODELS is synchronous and static; confirm this is acceptable given packages/ai/model-registry catalog updates require a deploy (not a runtime fetch) to reach the picker.

### Files

- Does document.body (or any ancestor of the Radix Dialog portal root) ever carry data-design="agi" at runtime? This pass found data-design="agi" applied only to specific content divs inside apps/web/app/chat/projects/[id]/page.tsx (lines 208/229/267/316) and apps/web/app/chat/projects/page.tsx:145, never on <html>/<body> or in any layout.tsx, and Dialog.tsx's DialogPrimitive.Portal is used with no container prop - if that holds, every var(--agi-\*) reference in KnowledgeFilesPanel.tsx and FilePreviewModal.tsx resolves to nothing (falls back to inherited/initial), which would need a live DOM inspection (devtools computed-style) to confirm, not just static analysis.
- Which of SourcesPanel vs. KnowledgeFilesPanel is the intended canonical knowledge-files surface, and is there a plan to consolidate them? Not resolved from code alone.
- The full per-provider translation of image_url/file parts (Anthropic content blocks, Gemini inlineData, etc.) was only traced to the anthropic-shape/openai-shape policy switch in packages/ai/provider-runtime/src/history.ts; the individual adapter files (packages/ai/providers/openai/src/translate.ts, translate-responses.ts) were located but not read in this pass.
- Whether the 12 MiB per-file / 12 MiB aggregate mismatch in the chat-attachment upload client is a known, accepted limitation (effectively capping useful batches to 1-2 files) or an unnoticed bug - no comment in managed-cloud-chat-attachments-client.ts addresses why the aggregate cap equals the per-file cap rather than e.g. 12 MiB x 10.
- apps/web/app/api/projects/[id]/knowledge-files/[fileId]/route.ts (the delete endpoint) was located via grep/import references but its body was not read in this pass - only inferred from its callers.

### Connectors

- Is the legacy ConnectorsPanel/SkillsPanel/PluginsPanel code in packages/ui/ui/src/settings-modal/SettingsModal.tsx still desktop's live UI, or also dead there -- worth confirming with apps/desktop before treating it as removable, since this map only verified apps/web's reachability.
- Was surfacing ConnectorScopeList / ConnectorCapabilitiesPanel / ConnectorConsentSummary on web's connector detail pane (via DirectoryAdapter.renderDetailFooter, the one footer slot web's ConnectorDetailView actually has) deliberately deferred, or is this an unnoticed regression from the DirectoryPanel migration -- the components, their tests, and their SettingsModal prop plumbing are all still present and current, which reads like an incomplete cutover rather than an intentional removal.
- Given ConnectorCapabilitiesPanel is currently the only caller of publishMcpContextSelection, is the composer's resource/prompt-attach chip (selectedMcpContext, ChatComposerNew.tsx:2785-2806) meant to be reachable at all on web today, or is it also mid-migration groundwork.
- Should useSkillsList's loading/error be surfaced in the slash-command menu (currently a fetch failure silently drops the skills section with no error row), or is that considered acceptable because built-in commands still render.
- Is DirectoryConnectorDetail.permissions (never populated) meant to replace or duplicate the separate ConnectorScopeList data path once/if the latter is wired back in -- two schemas for the same concept currently coexist.

### Search

- Did not trace the exact request-processor.ts/tool-loop dispatch point that reads researchEnabled from the client and routes into research-loop.ts vs. the normal single-turn tool loop -- research-loop.ts's existence and its bounds are confirmed, but the trigger condition itself was not read line-by-line.
- ResearchReportsGallery.tsx (Library tab) internals were not inspected -- unconfirmed whether it lists reports via a paginated endpoint distinct from /api/research/reports?limit=1, or reuses the same route with a higher limit.
- No setter for data-chat-theme='cool' was found anywhere in apps/web or packages/ui source; could not rule out it being set by an untracked build-time config, a desktop-app wrapper, or a future surface not yet in this search scope.
- GlobalSearchDialog's click-through behavior (navigating to a result, any 'highlightMessage' scroll/flash treatment in the transcript referenced in a WebChatPage.tsx comment) was not traced end-to-end.
- WEB_SEARCH_MAX_CALLS_PER_TURN=3 (generic Perplexity tool) is enforced server-side, but whether the UI surfaces any explicit feedback when a turn hits that budget (vs. simply stopping further calls silently) was not confirmed.

### Persistence

- This pass did not read apps/web/lib/server/rls-db.ts / getUserScopedDb() to confirm whether it now calls withUser()/SET LOCAL for the live query path -- 0037's own comment says it did not as of that migration, which matters for whether RLS is actually enforced today versus app-layer WHERE user_id=$1 doing all the work.
- Did not verify the content or retention-day cadence of apps/web/app/api/cron/purge-temporary-chats/route.ts and enforce-workspace-retention/route.ts beyond confirming they exist and reference the right columns/tables -- e.g. actual purge window for is_temporary conversations, and whether the org sweep cron is itself scheduled anywhere (crontab/Vercel cron config not inspected).
- Did not trace how desktop/mobile clients use the /api/chat/sync endpoint for conversations and messages (only confirmed the web app itself uses it solely for artifacts) -- if this map needs to speak to cross-surface sync fidelity, that requires reading the desktop/mobile sync clients too.
- Did not open apps/web/lib/server/neon-chat.ts (ChatMessageRow/ChatConversationRow types, normalizeMessageMetadata) or the ManagedCloudMessageMetadataSchema/ManagedCloudMessageWireSchema definitions in @agiworkforce/cloud-contracts in full -- the MessageMetadata TypeScript interface in the store was used as the primary source for 'what fields the client renders', which should agree with the wire schema but was not diffed field-by-field against it.
- Did not inspect UI-level empty/hover/focus/active states for the conversation list rows (sidebar) or the export dialog buttons -- those statesCovered entries reflect only what the persistence/store/API layer proves, not a visual audit of the sidebar components themselves.

### States

- Is the notification-store.ts toast/notification system (apps/web/shared/stores/notification-store.ts) intentionally dormant scaffolding for a future notification-center feature, or genuinely dead code that should be removed -- no ToastContainer/NotificationCenter component was found rendering its state anywhere in apps/web.
- Should WebChatPage's delete-conversation/delete-project flows be migrated from useConfirm to useConfirmAction to match the project rule doc and gain the busy/disabled-while-deleting affordance, or is useConfirm's fire-and-forget-then-close behavior intentional there.
- Are the packages/ui/unified-chat KeyboardShortcutsDialog.tsx / KeyboardShortcutsOverlay.tsx components genuinely unreachable dead exports from apps/web's perspective, or consumed by another surface (Chrome extension, VS Code) not audited here.
- Whether toUserMessage(error, String(error)) at WebChatPage.tsx:1833 and :2731 is a known, accepted gap in the raw-error guard for image/video generation failures specifically, or an oversight worth a follow-up fix plus a test-guard update.
- Whether there is a plan to bring any authenticated route (starting with /chat) under the a11y-audit.mjs script's coverage, given the script's own comment states it currently has no way to run signed in.

### Mobile

- No e2e spec re-measures ChatComposerNew's actual rendered pixel height, footer-entry count, or control-row button size at a real 390px (or any phone/tablet) browser viewport, the two dedicated test files that cover this behaviour (density.test.tsx, mobileFooter.test.tsx) are jsdom-only and each say outright, in their own comments, that jsdom cannot evaluate the media queries under test and 'the pixel outcome belongs to a browser run.' Per .claude/rules/ui-colour-and-interaction.md's own stated standard ('a jsdom pass is not a browser pass'), this looks like exactly the gap that rule was written to close, but I could not find the closing spec.
- No spec exercises the 641-768px band where the composer/panel breakpoint (640px) and the sidebar/header breakpoint (768px) disagree, is a ~700px viewport (a small Android tablet in portrait, or a folded foldable) an intentionally accepted mixed state, or an unconsidered gap?
- No test found for ArtifactsPanel's mobile modal-overlay branch (backdrop, role='dialog', focus containment) at either the component or e2e level, I could not confirm from tests alone that useOverlayLayout's mobile branch renders and behaves as documented; this is inferred from reading the component and hook source only.
- composer-editor.spec.ts and composer-editor-ime.spec.ts (mention picker, slash commands, IME composition) run only at the default desktop viewport; whether the mention/slash dropdown menus or IME candidate window interact correctly with the compact 36px input row or the on-screen keyboard is unverified by any spec I found.
- The follow-up-suggestions shell (ChatMessageList.tsx:1748) uses a flat px-4 gutter with no md:/lg: step, unlike the date-divider and typing-indicator rows two blocks above it in the same file (px-4 md:px-12 lg:px-20), I could not determine from the code alone whether this is intentional (the shell may already sit inside a max-w-3xl-constrained ancestor) or a drift from the surrounding pattern; would need a rendered-DOM check to resolve.
